
===== FUNCTION defaultDungeonRpgRules =====
function defaultDungeonRpgRules(){
  return {
    xpPerLevel:10,skillPointsPerLevel:1,startingSkillPoints:0,
    physicalDamageStep:10,physicalDamageGain:1,
    physicalDamageFormula:"step",physicalDamagePercentPerPoint:0,
    magicDamageStep:10,magicDamageGain:1,
    magicDamageFormula:"step",magicDamagePercentPerPoint:0,
    rangedDamageFormula:"none",rangedDamagePercentPerPoint:0,
    hpFormula:"step",hpPercentPerPoint:0,
    manaFormula:"step",manaPercentPerPoint:0,
    critFormula:"step",critPercentPerPoint:0,
    dodgeFormula:"step",dodgePercentPerPoint:0,
    initiativeFormula:"step",initiativePerPoint:0,
    rulesComplexity:"simple",initiativeUsage:"tests",initiativeTurnMode:"fixed",initiativeDie:20,testsEnabled:true,testDie:20,testDefaultDifficulty:10,
    magicResistFormula:"step",magicResistPerPoint:0,
    meleeHitStep:10,meleeHitGain:5,
    rangedHitStep:10,rangedHitGain:5,
    magicHitStep:10,magicHitGain:5,
    basicAttackBaseChance:50,
    baseMana:0,spiritManaStep:10,manaGain:10,magicAttackManaCost:1,
    baseCrit:0,agilityCritStep:10,critGain:5,critCap:50,critMultiplier:2,
    baseDodge:0,agilityDodgeStep:10,dodgeGain:3,dodgeCap:40,
    baseInitiative:0,agilityInitiativeStep:9999,initiativeGain:0,
    enduranceHpStep:10,hpGain:2,
    defensePenaltyPerPoint:1,hitChanceMin:5,hitChanceMax:95,
    armorReductionStep:2,armorReductionGain:1,minPhysicalDamage:1,
    spiritMagicResistStep:20,magicResistGain:1,minMagicDamage:1
  };
}

===== FUNCTION normalizeDungeonRpgRules =====
function normalizeDungeonRpgRules(r){
  const d=defaultDungeonRpgRules(),o={...d,...(r||{})};
  const pos=(k,min=1,max=9999)=>o[k]=Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Number(o[k])||d[k]));
  const non=(k,max=9999)=>o[k]=Math.max(0,Math.min(max,Number(o[k])||0));
  pos("xpPerLevel");non("skillPointsPerLevel",99);non("startingSkillPoints",99);
  pos("physicalDamageStep");non("physicalDamageGain",99);
  o.physicalDamageFormula=o.physicalDamageFormula==="percent"?"percent":"step";non("physicalDamagePercentPerPoint",100);
  pos("magicDamageStep");non("magicDamageGain",99);
  o.magicDamageFormula=o.magicDamageFormula==="percent"?"percent":"step";non("magicDamagePercentPerPoint",100);
  o.rangedDamageFormula=o.rangedDamageFormula==="percent"?"percent":"none";non("rangedDamagePercentPerPoint",100);
  o.hpFormula=o.hpFormula==="percent"?"percent":"step";non("hpPercentPerPoint",100);
  o.manaFormula=o.manaFormula==="percent"?"percent":"step";non("manaPercentPerPoint",100);
  o.critFormula=o.critFormula==="perPoint"?"perPoint":"step";non("critPercentPerPoint",100);
  o.dodgeFormula=o.dodgeFormula==="perPoint"?"perPoint":"step";non("dodgePercentPerPoint",100);
  o.initiativeFormula=o.initiativeFormula==="perPoint"?"perPoint":"step";non("initiativePerPoint",100);
  o.magicResistFormula=o.magicResistFormula==="perPoint"?"perPoint":"step";non("magicResistPerPoint",100);
  o.rulesComplexity=["simple","tactical","rpg","custom"].includes(o.rulesComplexity)?o.rulesComplexity:"simple";
  o.initiativeUsage=["off","turns","tests","both","custom"].includes(o.initiativeUsage)?o.initiativeUsage:"tests";
  o.initiativeTurnMode=["fixed","die","low"].includes(o.initiativeTurnMode)?o.initiativeTurnMode:"fixed";
  o.initiativeDie=[4,6,8,10,12,20,100].includes(Number(o.initiativeDie))?Number(o.initiativeDie):20;
  o.testsEnabled=o.testsEnabled!==false; o.testDie=[4,6,8,10,12,20,100].includes(Number(o.testDie))?Number(o.testDie):20;
  o.testDefaultDifficulty=Math.max(1,Math.min(999,Number(o.testDefaultDifficulty)||10));
  pos("meleeHitStep");non("meleeHitGain",99);
  pos("rangedHitStep");non("rangedHitGain",99);
  pos("magicHitStep");non("magicHitGain",99);
  o.basicAttackBaseChance=Math.max(1,Math.min(99,Number(o.basicAttackBaseChance)||d.basicAttackBaseChance));
  non("baseMana");pos("spiritManaStep");non("manaGain");non("magicAttackManaCost");
  non("baseCrit",100);pos("agilityCritStep");non("critGain",100);non("critCap",100);
  o.critMultiplier=Math.max(1,Math.min(10,Number(o.critMultiplier)||d.critMultiplier));
  non("baseDodge",100);pos("agilityDodgeStep");non("dodgeGain",100);non("dodgeCap",100);
  o.baseInitiative=Math.max(-99,Math.min(999,Number(o.baseInitiative)||0));
  pos("agilityInitiativeStep");non("initiativeGain",99);
  pos("enduranceHpStep");non("hpGain",999);
  non("defensePenaltyPerPoint",25);
  o.hitChanceMin=Math.max(0,Math.min(99,Number.isFinite(Number(o.hitChanceMin))?Number(o.hitChanceMin):d.hitChanceMin));
  o.hitChanceMax=Math.max(o.hitChanceMin,Math.min(100,Number(o.hitChanceMax)||d.hitChanceMax));
  pos("armorReductionStep");non("armorReductionGain",99);non("minPhysicalDamage",99);
  pos("spiritMagicResistStep");non("magicResistGain",99);non("minMagicDamage",99);
  return o;
}

===== FUNCTION fillDungeonRpgRulesEditor =====
function fillDungeonRpgRulesEditor(){
  const r=loadDungeonRpgRules(),map={
    drXpPerLevel:"xpPerLevel",drSkillPointsPerLevel:"skillPointsPerLevel",drStartingSkillPoints:"startingSkillPoints",
    drPhysicalStep:"physicalDamageStep",drPhysicalGain:"physicalDamageGain",
    drPhysicalPctPerPoint:"physicalDamagePercentPerPoint",
    drMagicStep:"magicDamageStep",drMagicGain:"magicDamageGain",
    drMagicPctPerPoint:"magicDamagePercentPerPoint",
    drRangedPctPerPoint:"rangedDamagePercentPerPoint",
    drHpPctPerPoint:"hpPercentPerPoint",drManaPctPerPoint:"manaPercentPerPoint",
    drCritPerPoint:"critPercentPerPoint",drDodgePerPoint:"dodgePercentPerPoint",
    drInitiativePerPoint:"initiativePerPoint",drMagicResistPerPoint:"magicResistPerPoint",
    drMeleeHitStep:"meleeHitStep",drMeleeHitGain:"meleeHitGain",
    drRangedHitStep:"rangedHitStep",drRangedHitGain:"rangedHitGain",
    drMagicHitStep:"magicHitStep",drMagicHitGain:"magicHitGain",
    drBasicHitChance:"basicAttackBaseChance",drHitChanceMin:"hitChanceMin",drHitChanceMax:"hitChanceMax",drDefensePenalty:"defensePenaltyPerPoint",
    drBaseMana:"baseMana",drSpiritStep:"spiritManaStep",drManaGain:"manaGain",drMagicAttackCost:"magicAttackManaCost",
    drBaseCrit:"baseCrit",drCritStep:"agilityCritStep",drCritGain:"critGain",drCritCap:"critCap",drCritMultiplier:"critMultiplier",
    drBaseDodge:"baseDodge",drDodgeStep:"agilityDodgeStep",drDodgeGain:"dodgeGain",drDodgeCap:"dodgeCap",
    drBaseInitiative:"baseInitiative",drInitiativeStep:"agilityInitiativeStep",drInitiativeGain:"initiativeGain",
    drHpStep:"enduranceHpStep",drHpGain:"hpGain",
    drArmorStep:"armorReductionStep",drArmorGain:"armorReductionGain",drMinPhysicalDamage:"minPhysicalDamage",
    drMagicResistStep:"spiritMagicResistStep",drMagicResistGain:"magicResistGain",drMinMagicDamage:"minMagicDamage"
  };
  Object.entries(map).forEach(([id,k])=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=r[k]});
  const selects={
    drPhysicalFormula:r.physicalDamageFormula,drMagicFormula:r.magicDamageFormula,
    drRangedDamageFormula:r.rangedDamageFormula,drHpFormula:r.hpFormula,drManaFormula:r.manaFormula,
    drCritFormula:r.critFormula,drDodgeFormula:r.dodgeFormula,drInitiativeFormula:r.initiativeFormula,
    drMagicResistFormula:r.magicResistFormula
  };
  Object.entries(selects).forEach(([id,val])=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=val});
  [["drRulesComplexity",r.rulesComplexity],["drInitiativeUsage",r.initiativeUsage],["drInitiativeTurnMode",r.initiativeTurnMode],["drInitiativeDie",r.initiativeDie],["drTestDie",r.testDie]].forEach(([id,val])=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=String(val)});
  const te=document.getElementById("drTestsEnabled");if(te)te.checked=r.testsEnabled!==false; const td=document.getElementById("drTestDefaultDifficulty");if(td)td.value=r.testDefaultDifficulty; renderDungeonProgressiveRules();
  const p=document.getElementById("dungeonRulesEditorPreview");if(p)p.innerHTML="<strong>📖 Résumé joueur</strong>"+dungeonRpgRulesSummaryHtml();
}

===== FUNCTION readDungeonRpgRulesEditor =====
function readDungeonRpgRulesEditor(){
  const v=id=>Number(document.getElementById(id)?.value);
  return {

    xpPerLevel:v("drXpPerLevel"),skillPointsPerLevel:v("drSkillPointsPerLevel"),startingSkillPoints:v("drStartingSkillPoints"),
    physicalDamageStep:v("drPhysicalStep"),physicalDamageGain:v("drPhysicalGain"),
    physicalDamageFormula:document.getElementById("drPhysicalFormula")?.value||"step",physicalDamagePercentPerPoint:v("drPhysicalPctPerPoint"),
    magicDamageStep:v("drMagicStep"),magicDamageGain:v("drMagicGain"),
    magicDamageFormula:document.getElementById("drMagicFormula")?.value||"step",magicDamagePercentPerPoint:v("drMagicPctPerPoint"),
    rangedDamageFormula:document.getElementById("drRangedDamageFormula")?.value||"none",rangedDamagePercentPerPoint:v("drRangedPctPerPoint"),
    hpFormula:document.getElementById("drHpFormula")?.value||"step",hpPercentPerPoint:v("drHpPctPerPoint"),
    manaFormula:document.getElementById("drManaFormula")?.value||"step",manaPercentPerPoint:v("drManaPctPerPoint"),
    critFormula:document.getElementById("drCritFormula")?.value||"step",critPercentPerPoint:v("drCritPerPoint"),
    dodgeFormula:document.getElementById("drDodgeFormula")?.value||"step",dodgePercentPerPoint:v("drDodgePerPoint"),
    initiativeFormula:document.getElementById("drInitiativeFormula")?.value||"step",initiativePerPoint:v("drInitiativePerPoint"),
    magicResistFormula:document.getElementById("drMagicResistFormula")?.value||"step",magicResistPerPoint:v("drMagicResistPerPoint"),
    meleeHitStep:v("drMeleeHitStep"),meleeHitGain:v("drMeleeHitGain"),
    rangedHitStep:v("drRangedHitStep"),rangedHitGain:v("drRangedHitGain"),
    magicHitStep:v("drMagicHitStep"),magicHitGain:v("drMagicHitGain"),
    basicAttackBaseChance:v("drBasicHitChance"),hitChanceMin:v("drHitChanceMin"),hitChanceMax:v("drHitChanceMax"),defensePenaltyPerPoint:v("drDefensePenalty"),
    baseMana:v("drBaseMana"),spiritManaStep:v("drSpiritStep"),manaGain:v("drManaGain"),magicAttackManaCost:v("drMagicAttackCost"),
    baseCrit:v("drBaseCrit"),agilityCritStep:v("drCritStep"),critGain:v("drCritGain"),critCap:v("drCritCap"),critMultiplier:v("drCritMultiplier"),
    baseDodge:v("drBaseDodge"),agilityDodgeStep:v("drDodgeStep"),dodgeGain:v("drDodgeGain"),dodgeCap:v("drDodgeCap"),
    baseInitiative:v("drBaseInitiative"),agilityInitiativeStep:v("drInitiativeStep"),initiativeGain:v("drInitiativeGain"),
    enduranceHpStep:v("drHpStep"),hpGain:v("drHpGain"),
    armorReductionStep:v("drArmorStep"),armorReductionGain:v("drArmorGain"),minPhysicalDamage:v("drMinPhysicalDamage"),
    spiritMagicResistStep:v("drMagicResistStep"),magicResistGain:v("drMagicResistGain"),minMagicDamage:v("drMinMagicDamage")
  };
}

===== FUNCTION saveDungeonRpgRulesFromEditor =====
function saveDungeonRpgRulesFromEditor(){saveDungeonRpgRules(readDungeonRpgRulesEditor());fillDungeonRpgRulesEditor();alert("Règles RPG Dungeon enregistrées ✅")}

===== FUNCTION currentRpgEconomy =====
function currentRpgEconomy(){
  try{
    const editorOpen=document.getElementById("rpgUniverseEditorModal")?.style.display==="block";
    if(editorOpen && typeof currentRpgProfile==="function"){
      const p=currentRpgProfile();
      if(p?.rpgUniverse?.economy)return p.rpgUniverse.economy;
    }
  }catch(e){}
  try{
    const p=getActiveGameProfile?.();
    if(p?.rpgUniverse?.economy)return p.rpgUniverse.economy;
  }catch(e){}
  return gensBlankRpgSettings().economy;
}

===== FUNCTION saveRpgUniverseEconomy =====
function saveRpgUniverseEconomy(){
  const profiles=loadGameProfiles(),i=profiles.findIndex(x=>String(x.id)===String(rpgEditingId));if(i<0)return;
  const p=profiles[i];ensureRpgProfileData(p);const e=p.rpgUniverse.economy;
  e.enabled=!!p.rpgUniverse.gameplay?.modules?.economy;
  e.currencyName=(document.getElementById("rpgCurrencyName")?.value||"Or").trim()||"Or";
  e.currencyIcon=(document.getElementById("rpgCurrencyIcon")?.value||"🪙").trim()||"🪙";
  e.startingGold=Math.max(0,Number(document.getElementById("rpgStartingGold")?.value)||0);
  e.sellPercent=Math.max(0,Number(document.getElementById("rpgSellPercent")?.value)||0);
  e.merchantBuyMultiplier=Math.max(0,Number(document.getElementById("rpgMerchantBuyMultiplier")?.value)||1);
  e.enemyGoldEnabled=document.getElementById("rpgEnemyGoldEnabled")?.value!=="false";
  e.merchantEventEnabled=document.getElementById("rpgMerchantEventEnabled")?.value!=="false";
  saveGameProfiles(profiles);applyGameProfile(p,false);renderRpgUniverseEditor();alert("Économie enregistrée ✅");
}

===== FUNCTION merchantItems =====
function merchantItems(){
  refreshCustomEquipmentIntoItems();
  const captureOn=!!(gensGameplayModules?.()?.capture);
  return ITEMS.filter(it=>
    dungeonMerchantDefaultPrice066(it)>0 &&
    (!isDungeonMode()||isDungeonItemRecord(it)) &&
    (!isDungeonMode() || (!it.lootOnly && String(it.type||"").toLowerCase()!=="butin")) &&
    (captureOn || it.type!=="Objet de capture")
  );
}

===== FUNCTION openMerchant =====
function openMerchant(){
  if(!gensEconomyEnabled()){alert("Le système économique est désactivé dans cet univers.");return}
  if(!current){const ids=dungeonParticipants?.()||[];if(ids.length)current=ids[0]}
  renderMerchant();const m=document.getElementById("merchantModal");if(m)m.style.display="block";
}

===== FUNCTION renderMerchant =====
function renderMerchant(){
  const box=document.getElementById("merchantList"),wallet=document.getElementById("merchantWallet");if(!box)return;
  const e=currentRpgEconomy(),gold=current?gensHeroGold(current):0;
  if(wallet)wallet.innerHTML='<strong>'+gensCurrencyLabel(gold)+'</strong> · Prix achat ×'+Number(e.merchantBuyMultiplier||1)+' · Revente '+Number(e.sellPercent||0)+'%';
  const items=merchantItems();
  box.innerHTML=items.length?items.map(it=>{const buy=Math.max(0,Math.round(dungeonMerchantDefaultPrice066(it)*(Number(e.merchantBuyMultiplier)||1)));return '<div class="v2LibCard"><div class="v2LibHead"><strong>'+z40kEscHtml(it.name||it.id)+'</strong><span class="badge">'+gensCurrencyLabel(buy)+'</span></div><div class="small">'+z40kEscHtml(it.type||"Objet")+'</div><div class="v2LibActions"><button type="button" onclick="merchantBuy(\''+z40kEscAttr(it.id)+'\')">🛒 ACHETER</button></div></div>'}).join(""):'<div class="empty">Aucun objet avec une valeur marchande.</div>';
  if(current){
    const st=loadState(current),inv=Array.isArray(st.inventory)?st.inventory:[];
    if(inv.length)box.innerHTML+='<h3 style="margin:12px 0 0">💸 Vendre</h3>'+inv.map((x,i)=>{const it=itemById(x.itemId||x.id),sell=Math.max(0,Math.round((Number(it?.price)||0)*(Number(e.sellPercent)||0)/100));return it&&sell>0?'<div class="v2LibCard"><div class="v2LibHead"><strong>'+z40kEscHtml(it.name)+'</strong><span class="badge">'+gensCurrencyLabel(sell)+'</span></div><div class="v2LibActions"><button type="button" onclick="merchantSell('+i+')">💸 VENDRE</button></div></div>':''}).join("");
  }
}

===== FUNCTION merchantBuy =====
function merchantBuy(itemId){
  if(!current)return;const it=itemById(itemId);if(!it)return;const e=currentRpgEconomy(),price=Math.max(0,Math.round(dungeonMerchantDefaultPrice066(it)*(Number(e.merchantBuyMultiplier)||1)));
  if(gensHeroGold(current)<price){alert("Pas assez de "+(e.currencyName||"Or")+".");return}
  const st=loadState(current);st.inventory=Array.isArray(st.inventory)?st.inventory:[];st.inventory.push(makeInventoryEntry(itemId));st.gold=gensHeroGold(current)-price;localStorage.setItem(key(current),JSON.stringify(st));state=st;renderMerchant();try{renderInventory();renderEconomyWallet()}catch(e){}
}

===== FUNCTION merchantSell =====
function merchantSell(index){
  if(!current)return;const st=loadState(current);st.inventory=Array.isArray(st.inventory)?st.inventory:[];const entry=st.inventory[index],it=itemById(entry?.itemId||entry?.id);if(!it)return;
  const price=Math.max(0,Math.round((Number(it.price)||0)*(Number(currentRpgEconomy().sellPercent)||0)/100));if(price<=0)return;
  st.inventory.splice(index,1);st.gold=gensHeroGold(current)+price;localStorage.setItem(key(current),JSON.stringify(st));state=st;renderMerchant();try{renderInventory();renderHands();renderGear();renderEconomyWallet()}catch(e){}
}

===== FUNCTION dungeonSetHeroWounds =====
function dungeonSetHeroWounds(heroId,delta){
  if(!heroId)return null;
  const s=loadState(heroId),max=dungeonMaxHpForHero(heroId,s);
  s.wounds=Math.max(0,Math.min(max,(Number(s.wounds)||0)+Number(delta||0)));
  localStorage.setItem(key(heroId),JSON.stringify(s));
  if(current===heroId){state=s;try{render()}catch(e){}}
  return s.wounds;
}

===== FUNCTION applyDamage48 =====
function applyDamage48(heroId,damage){
  damage=Math.max(1,Number(damage)||1);
  const before=dungeonDerivedForHero?.(heroId)?.hp;
  dungeonSetHeroWounds?.(heroId,damage);
  const after=dungeonDerivedForHero?.(heroId)?.hp;
  try{DungeonCore01?.render?.()}catch(e){}
  return {before,after,damage:Math.max(0,(Number(before)||0)-(Number(after)||0))||damage};
}

===== FUNCTION applyBossAttackDamage =====
function applyBossAttackDamage(instanceId,damage,armorRolls,threshold){
  const list=loadActiveEnemies();
  const inst=list.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(instanceId));if(!inst)return;
  const def=activeEnemyDefinition(inst.enemyId);if(!def)return;
  const before=Math.max(0,Number(inst.hp)||0);
  const wasDefeated=!!inst.defeated;
  inst.hp=Math.max(0,before-Math.max(0,Number(damage)||0));
  const phase=checkEnemyPhases(inst,def);
  if(Number(damage)>0)triggerBossReaction(inst,def,"damaged");
  if(phase)triggerBossReaction(inst,def,"phase");
  inst.defeated=inst.hp<=0;
  let drops=[],xpReward={total:0,shares:[]};
  if(inst.defeated&&!wasDefeated){
    const killerId=attackRollContext?.heroId||combatRewardHeroId||current;
    drops=rollEnemyLoot(inst,def);
    xpReward=awardDungeonDefeatXp(def,killerId);
    dungeonRecordCombatReward(def,xpReward,drops,killerId);
  }
  if(inst.defeated)saveActiveEnemies(list.filter(x=>x.id!==inst.id)); else saveActiveEnemies(list);
  renderActiveEnemies();

  const saved=armorRolls.length?armorRolls.filter(v=>v>=threshold).length:0;
  document.getElementById("attackDiceResult").innerHTML=
    '<div class="bossCombatResult"><strong>👑 '+z40kEscHtml(def.name)+'</strong><br>'+
    (armorRolls.length?'🛡️ '+saved+' sauvegardée'+(saved>1?'s':'')+' / '+armorRolls.length+'<br>':'')+
    '💥 <strong>'+damage+' dégât'+(damage>1?'s':'')+'</strong><br>'+
    '❤️ '+before+' → <strong>'+inst.hp+'</strong> / '+inst.maxHp+
    (inst.defeated?'<br>☠️ <strong>'+((String(def.category||"").toLowerCase().includes("boss"))?"BOSS VAINCU":"ENNEMI VAINCU")+'</strong>':'')+(inst.defeated?'<br>'+dungeonDefeatRewardText(def,attackRollContext?.heroId||combatRewardHeroId||current,xpReward,drops):'')+'</div>';

  if(phase){
    showEffectPopupDelayed("👑",phase.name||"NOUVELLE PHASE",
      z40kEscHtml(phase.text||"Le comportement du Boss change."),
      def.name+" · "+Math.round((inst.hp/Math.max(1,inst.maxHp))*100)+"% PV",500,true);
  }
  if(drops.length){
    const txt=drops.map(d=>"🎁 "+z40kEscHtml(d.name)+" ×"+d.qty).join("<br>");
    const given=grantEnemyLootToCurrentHero(drops);
    showEffectPopupDelayed("🎁","LOOT !",txt,
      given?"Ajouté à l’inventaire de "+(CHARS[current]?.name||"héros")+".":"Drop obtenu — attribue-le manuellement à un héros.",900,true);
  }
}

===== FUNCTION baseArmorSave =====
function baseArmorSave(){
  const chosen=Number(document.getElementById("armorBase")?.value||0);
  if(chosen)return chosen;
  const c=CHARS[current]||{};
  return Number(c.armorSave)||2;
}

===== FUNCTION explore =====
function explore(){if(!canAct()){modal('⏳ Action refusée','Ce n’est pas le tour du héros contrôlé par ce téléphone.');return}const living=dungeonLivingEnemies?.()||[];const exitLocked=living.length>0 && (typeof window.dungeonRoomExitLocked102==="function"?window.dungeonRoomExitLocked102():true);if(exitLocked){modal('🔐 Sortie verrouillée','La sortie de cette salle est verrouillée. La clé est portée par un ennemi : éliminez le porteur pour poursuivre.');return}const cfg=loadDungeonConfig(),s=loadDungeonState();if((Number(s.room)||0)>=Number(cfg.rooms||0)){modal('🏆 Donjon terminé','Vous avez exploré les '+Number(cfg.rooms||0)+' salles du donjon.');return}s.room=Math.max(0,Number(s.room)||0)+1;const room=s.room;saveDungeonState(s);let kind=null;const explicitBossRooms=String(cfg.bossRooms||'').split(/[,; ]+/).map(Number).filter(n=>Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0);if(cfg.boss==='final'&&room===cfg.rooms)kind='boss';else if(cfg.boss==='everyN'&&room%Math.max(1,Number(cfg.bossEvery)||5)===0)kind='boss';else if(cfg.boss==='specific'&&explicitBossRooms.includes(room))kind='boss';else if(cfg.boss==='random'&&room>2&&Math.random()<(Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(cfg.bossChance)||13))/100))kind='boss';if(!kind){const w={enemy:44,ambush:12,trap:14,chest:14,merchant:8,rest:11,mystery:11,...(cfg.roomWeights||{})};const entries=[['enemy',w.enemy],['ambush',w.ambush],['trap',w.trap],['chest',w.chest],['merchant',w.merchant],['rest',w.rest],['mystery',w.mystery]].map(([k,v])=>[k,Math.max(0,Number(v)||0)]);let r=Math.random()*(entries.reduce((n,x)=>n+x[1],0)||1);kind='enemy';for(const [k,v] of entries){r-=v;if(r<=0){kind=k;break}}}
 let result;if(kind==='enemy'){
  result=dungeonEncounter(room);
  result.text='<strong>⚔️ Des ennemis occupent la salle.</strong><br>Ferme cette fenêtre puis utilise <strong>ENGAGER LE COMBAT</strong> lorsque tu es prêt.<br><br>'+result.text;
}else if(kind==='ambush'){result=dungeonEncounter(room);result.title='⚡ EMBUSCADE — '+String(result.title||'Rencontre').replace(/^⚔️\s*Rencontre\s*—\s*/,'');result.text='<strong>⚠️ EMBUSCADE : les ennemis surprennent le groupe.</strong><br>La règle de proximité est ignorée pour cette rencontre : le combat peut être lancé immédiatement.<br><br>'+result.text;}else if(kind==='boss')result=dungeonBossRoom(room);else if(kind==='trap'){const trap=dungeonPickTrapType(cfg);result={title:'🪤 '+trap.name,text:'Un piège est présent dans la salle. En mode tactique, il ne se déclenche qu’au contact de sa case.',enemyQty:0,enemyName:'',trapId:trap.id}}else if(kind==='chest'){const rarity=dungeonPickChestRarity(cfg),trapped=Math.random()*100<Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(cfg.chestTrapChance)||0));const el=addDungeonSceneElement({kind:'chest',name:'Coffre '+dungeonRarityLabel(rarity),rarity,trapped,room});result={title:'🎁 '+(el?.name||'Coffre découvert'),text:'Un coffre <strong>'+z40kEscHtml(dungeonRarityLabel(rarity).toLowerCase())+'</strong> est présent dans la salle.'+(trapped?'<br>⚠️ Il peut être piégé.':''),enemyQty:0,enemyName:''}}else if(kind==='merchant'){const el=addDungeonSceneElement({kind:'merchant',name:'Marchand itinérant',room});result={title:'🧙‍♂️ Marchand itinérant',text:'Un marchand a installé son étal dans cette salle. Le groupe peut commercer avant de repartir.',enemyQty:0,enemyName:''}}else if(kind==='rest'){
  const ex=(getActiveGameProfile?.()?.rpgUniverse?.exploration)||{},restOn=ex.restEnabled!==false,heal=restOn?Math.max(0,Number(ex.restHeal)||0):0,mana=restOn?Math.max(0,Number(ex.restMana)||0):0,notes=[];
  dungeonParticipants().forEach(id=>{
    if(heal>0){dungeonSetHeroWounds(id,-heal);notes.push((CHARS[id]?.name||id)+' +'+heal+' PV')}
    if(mana>0){const hs=loadState(id),oc=window.current,os=window.state;try{window.current=id;window.state=hs;hs.mana=Math.min(dungeonMaxMana(),Math.max(0,Number(hs.mana)||0)+mana)}finally{window.current=oc;window.state=os}localStorage.setItem(key(id),JSON.stringify(hs))}
  });
  result={title:'⛩️ Sanctuaire de repos',text:(heal||mana)?('Le sanctuaire restaure le groupe.<br><strong>PV : +'+heal+' · Mana : +'+mana+'</strong>'):'Un sanctuaire ancien offre un lieu sûr, sans restauration automatique.',enemyQty:0,enemyName:''}
}else result={title:'✨ Salle calme',text:'La salle est vide. Le groupe peut souffler avant de poursuivre.',enemyQty:0,enemyName:''};const narrative=cfg.narration!==false?dungeonNarrativeText(kind,result.enemyName||'une créature',result.enemyQty||1,room):'';const map=cfg.map!==false?generateDungeonMap(kind,result.enemyQty||0):null;s.last={kind,room,at:Date.now(),narrative,map,title:result.title};saveDungeonState(s);try{dc077MaybeCreateObjective?.(room,kind)}catch(e){}const mapText=map?'<div class="dc01Map"><strong>🗺️ Configuration</strong><br>'+dungeonMapHtml(map)+'</div>':'';try{queueDungeonRoomEvent074?.(room,kind)}catch(e){}const ambushAction=kind==='ambush'
  ?'<button class="dc01Btn primary dc076AmbushBtn" style="width:100%;margin-top:12px" onclick="dc076StartAmbushCombat()">⚔️ COMMENCER LE COMBAT</button>'
  :'';
modal('🏰 SALLE '+room+' / '+cfg.rooms+' — '+result.title,(narrative?'<div class="dc01Notice">'+z40kEscHtml(narrative)+'</div>':'')+result.text+mapText+ambushAction);
render();
}

--- minPhysicalDamage in defaultDungeonRpgRules ---
function defaultDungeonRpgRules(){
  return {
    xpPerLevel:10,skillPointsPerLevel:1,startingSkillPoints:0,
    physicalDamageStep:10,physicalDamageGain:1,
    physicalDamageFormula:"step",physicalDamagePercentPerPoint:0,
    magicDamageStep:10,magicDamageGain:1,
    magicDamageFormula:"step",magicDamagePercentPerPoint:0,
    rangedDamageFormula:"none",rangedDamagePercentPerPoint:0,
    hpFormula:"step",hpPercentPerPoint:0,
    manaFormula:"step",manaPercentPerPoint:0,
    critFormula:"step",critPercentPerPoint:0,
    dodgeFormula:"step",dodgePercentPerPoint:0,
    initiativeFormula:"step",initiativePerPoint:0,
    rulesComplexity:"simple",initiativeUsage:"tests",initiativeTurnMode:"fixed",initiativeDie:20,testsEnabled:true,testDie:20,testDefaultDifficulty:10,
    magicResistFormula:"step",magicResistPerPoint:0,
    meleeHitStep:10,meleeHitGain:5,
    rangedHitStep:10,rangedHitGain:5,
    magicHitStep:10,magicHitGain:5,
    basicAttackBaseChance:50,
    baseMana:0,spiritManaStep:10,manaGain:10,magicAttackManaCost:1,
    baseCrit:0,agilityCritStep:10,critGain:5,critCap:50,critMultiplier:2,
    baseDodge:0,agilityDodgeStep:10,dodgeGain:3,dodgeCap:40,
    baseInitiative:0,agilityInitiativeStep:9999,initiativeGain:0,
    enduranceHpStep:10,hpGain:2,
    defensePenaltyPerPoint:1,hitChanceMin:5,hitChanceMax:95,
    armorReductionStep:2,armorReductionGain:1,minPhysicalDamage:1,
    spiritMagicResistStep:20,magicResistGain:1,minMagicDamage:1
  };
}

--- minPhysicalDamage in normalizeDungeonRpgRules ---
function normalizeDungeonRpgRules(r){
  const d=defaultDungeonRpgRules(),o={...d,...(r||{})};
  const pos=(k,min=1,max=9999)=>o[k]=Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Number(o[k])||d[k]));
  const non=(k,max=9999)=>o[k]=Math.max(0,Math.min(max,Number(o[k])||0));
  pos("xpPerLevel");non("skillPointsPerLevel",99);non("startingSkillPoints",99);
  pos("physicalDamageStep");non("physicalDamageGain",99);
  o.physicalDamageFormula=o.physicalDamageFormula==="percent"?"percent":"step";non("physicalDamagePercentPerPoint",100);
  pos("magicDamageStep");non("magicDamageGain",99);
  o.magicDamageFormula=o.magicDamageFormula==="percent"?"percent":"step";non("magicDamagePercentPerPoint",100);
  o.rangedDamageFormula=o.rangedDamageFormula==="percent"?"percent":"none";non("rangedDamagePercentPerPoint",100);
  o.hpFormula=o.hpFormula==="percent"?"percent":"step";non("hpPercentPerPoint",100);
  o.manaFormula=o.manaFormula==="percent"?"percent":"step";non("manaPercentPerPoint",100);
  o.critFormula=o.critFormula==="perPoint"?"perPoint":"step";non("critPercentPerPoint",100);
  o.dodgeFormula=o.dodgeFormula==="perPoint"?"perPoint":"step";non("dodgePercentPerPoint",100);
  o.initiativeFormula=o.initiativeFormula==="perPoint"?"perPoint":"step";non("initiativePerPoint",100);
  o.magicResistFormula=o.magicResistFormula==="perPoint"?"perPoint":"step";non("magicResistPerPoint",100);
  o.rulesComplexity=["simple","tactical","rpg","custom"].includes(o.rulesComplexity)?o.rulesComplexity:"simple";
  o.initiativeUsage=["off","turns","tests","both","custom"].includes(o.initiativeUsage)?o.initiativeUsage:"tests";
  o.initiativeTurnMode=["fixed","die","low"].includes(o.initiativeTurnMode)?o.initiativeTurnMode:"fixed";
  o.initiativeDie=[4,6,8,10,12,20,100].includes(Number(o.initiativeDie))?Number(o.initiativeDie):20;
  o.testsEnabled=o.testsEnabled!==false; o.testDie=[4,6,8,10,12,20,100].includes(Number(o.testDie))?Number(o.testDie):20;
  o.testDefaultDifficulty=Math.max(1,Math.min(999,Number(o.testDefaultDifficulty)||10));
  pos("meleeHitStep");non("meleeHitGain",99);
  pos("rangedHitStep");non("rangedHitGain",99);
  pos("magicHitStep");non("magicHitGain",99);
  o.basicAttackBaseChance=Math.max(1,Math.min(99,Number(o.basicAttackBaseChance)||d.basicAttackBaseChance));
  non("baseMana");pos("spiritManaStep");non("manaGain");non("magicAttackManaCost");
  non("baseCrit",100);pos("agilityCritStep");non("critGain",100);non("critCap",100);
  o.critMultiplier=Math.max(1,Math.min(10,Number(o.critMultiplier)||d.critMultiplier));
  non("baseDodge",100);pos("agilityDodgeStep");non("dodgeGain",100);non("dodgeCap",100);
  o.baseInitiative=Math.max(-99,Math.min(999,Number(o.baseInitiative)||0));
  pos("agilityInitiativeStep");non("initiativeGain",99);
  pos("enduranceHpStep");non("hpGain",999);
  non("defensePenaltyPerPoint",25);
  o.hitChanceMin=Math.max(0,Math.min(99,Number.isFinite(Number(o.hitChanceMin))?Number(o.hitChanceMin):d.hitChanceMin));
  o.hitChanceMax=Math.max(o.hitChanceMin,Math.min(100,Number(o.hitChanceMax)||d.hitChanceMax));
  pos("armorReductionStep");non("armorReductionGain",99);non("minPhysicalDamage",99);
  pos("spiritMagicResistStep");non("magicResistGain",99);non("minMagicDamage",99);
  return o;
}

    r.magicResistFormula==="perPoint"

function fillDungeonRpgRulesEditor(){
  const r=loadDungeonRpgRules(),map={
    drXpPerLevel:"xpPerLevel",drSkillPointsPerLevel:"skillPointsPerLevel",drStartingSkillPoints:"startingSkillPoints",
    drPhysicalStep:"physicalDamageStep",drPhysicalGain:"physicalDamageGain",
    drPhysicalPctPerPoint:"physicalDamagePercentPerPoint",
    drMagicStep:"magicDamageStep",drMagicGain:"magicDamageGain",
    drMagicPctPerPoint:"magicDamagePercentPerPoint",
    drRangedPctPerPoint:"rangedDamagePercentPerPoint",
    drHpPctPerPoint:"hpPercentPerPoint",drManaPctPerPoint:"manaPercentPerPoint",
    drCritPerPoint:"critPercentPerPoint",drDodgePerPoint:"dodgePercentPerPoint",
    drInitiativePerPoint:"initiativePerPoint",drMagicResistPerPoint:"magicResistPerPoint",
    drMeleeHitStep:"meleeHitStep",drMeleeHitGain:"meleeHitGain",
    drRangedHitStep:"rangedHitStep",drRangedHitGain:"rangedHitGain",
    drMagicHitStep:"magicHitStep",drMagicHitGain:"magicHitGain",
    drBasicHitChance:"basicAttackBaseChance",drHitChanceMin:"hitChanceMin",drHitChanceMax:"hitChanceMax",drDefensePenalty:"defensePenaltyPerPoint",
    drBaseMana:"baseMana",drSpiritStep:"spiritManaStep",drManaGain:"manaGain",drMagicAttackCost:"magicAttackManaCost",
    drBaseCrit:"baseCrit",drCritStep:"agilityCritStep",drCritGain:"critGain",drCritCap:"critCap",drCritMultiplier:"critMultiplier",
    drBaseDodge:"baseDodge",drDodgeStep:"agilityDodgeStep",drDodgeGain:"dodgeGain",drDodgeCap:"dodgeCap",
    drBaseInitiative:"baseInitiative",drInitiativeStep:"agilityInitiativeStep",drInitiativeGain:"initiativeGain",
    drHpStep:"enduranceHpStep",drHpGain:"hpGain",
    drArmorStep:"armorReductionStep",drArmorGain:"armorReductionGain",drMinPhysicalDamage:"minPhysicalDamage",
    drMagicResistStep:"spiritMagicResistStep",drMagicResistGain:"magicResistGain",drMinMagicDamage:"minMagicDamage"
  };
  Object.entries(map).forEach(([id,k])=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=r[k]});
  const selects={
    drPhysicalFormula:r.physicalDamageFormula,drMagicFormula:r.magicDamageFormula,
    drRangedDamageFormula:r.rangedDamageFormula,drHpFormula:r.hpFormula,drManaFormula:r.manaFormula,
    drCritFormula:r.critFormula,drDodgeFormula:r.dodgeFormula,drInitiativeFormula:r.initiativeFormula,
    drMagicResistFormula:r.magicResistFormula
  };
  Object.entries(selects).forEach(([id,val])=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=val});
  [["drRulesComplexity",r.rulesComplexity],["drInitiativeUsage",r.initiativeUsage],["drInitiativeTurnMode",r.initiativeTurnMode],["drInitiativeDie",r.initiativeDie],["drTestDie",r.testDie]].forEach(([id,val])=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=String(val)});
  const te=document.getElementById("drTestsEnabled");if(te)te.checked=r.testsEnabled!==false; const td=document.getElementById("drTestDefaultDifficulty");if(td)td.value=r.testDefaultDifficulty; renderDungeonProgressiveRules();
  const p=document.getElementById("dungeonRulesEditorPreview");if(p)p.innerHTML="<strong>📖 Résumé joueur</strong>"+dungeonRpgRulesSummaryHtml();
}

--- minPhysicalDamage in syncRpgUniverseToDungeonRules ---
function syncRpgUniverseToDungeonRules(profile){
  if(!profile||profile.gameStyle!=="dungeon"||!profile.rpgUniverse)return;
  ensureRpgProfileData(profile);
  const u=profile.rpgUniverse,p=u.progression||{},st=u.stats||{},c=u.combat||{},r=loadDungeonRpgRules();
  saveDungeonRpgRules({...r,
    xpPerLevel:Number(p.xpPerLevel)||10,skillPointsPerLevel:Math.max(0,Number(p.talentPointsPerLevel)||0),startingSkillPoints:Math.max(0,Number(p.startingSkillPoints)||0),
    enduranceHpStep:10,hpGain:Math.max(0,Number(st.hpPer10Endurance)||0),baseMana:Math.max(0,Number(st.baseMana)||0),spiritManaStep:10,manaGain:Math.max(0,Number(st.manaPer10Spirit)||0),
    basicAttackBaseChance:c.baseHit,hitChanceMin:c.minHit,hitChanceMax:c.maxHit,critMultiplier:c.critMultiplier,critCap:c.critCap,minPhysicalDamage:c.minPhysicalDamage,minMagicDamage:c.minMagicDamage,
    physicalDamageFormula:c.physicalDamageFormula||"step",physicalDamagePercentPerPoint:Number(c.physicalDamagePercentPerPoint)||0,magicDamageFormula:c.magicDamageFormula||"step",magicDamagePercentPerPoint:Number(c.magicDamagePercentPerPoint)||0,rangedDamageFormula:c.rangedDamageFormula||"none",rangedDamagePercentPerPoint:Number(c.rangedDamagePercentPerPoint)||0,hpFormula:c.hpFormula||"step",hpPercentPerPoint:Number(c.hpPercentPerPoint)||0,manaFormula:c.manaFormula||"step",manaPercentPerPoint:Number(c.manaPercentPerPoint)||0,critFormula:c.critFormula||"step",critPercentPerPoint:Number(c.critPercentPerPoint)||0,dodgeFormula:c.dodgeFormula||"step",dodgePercentPerPoint:Number(c.dodgePercentPerPoint)||0,
    initiativeFormula:"direct",initiativePerPoint:0,baseInitiative:0,agilityInitiativeStep:9999,initiativeGain:0,
    rulesComplexity:c.rulesComplexity||"simple",initiativeUsage:c.initiativeUsage||"tests",initiativeTurnMode:c.initiativeTurnMode||"fixed",initiativeDie:Number(c.initiativeDie)||20,testsEnabled:c.testsEnabled!==false,testDie:Number(c.testDie)||20,testDefaultDifficulty:Number(c.testDefaultDifficulty)||10,
    magicResistFormula:c.magicResistFormula||"step",magicResistPerPoint:Number(c.magicResistPerPoint)||0
  });
}

function gensReferenceRpgSettings(){
  const rules=loadDungeonRpgRules();
  return {
    icon:"🧭",themeColor:"#654c32",
    gameplay:{profile:"custom",modules:{
      heroCombat:true,controllableCreatures:false,capture:false,companions:false,equipment:true,
      elements:false,economy:false,mana:true,progression:true,evolution:false,
      permadeath:false,ko:true,talentTrees:true,movement:false
    }},

function saveRpgUniverseCombat(){

--- minPhysicalDamage in dungeonMitigationForHero ---
function dungeonMitigationForHero(heroId,damageType){
  const d=dungeonDerivedForHero(heroId),r=loadDungeonRpgRules();
  const passive=Math.max(0,dungeonHeroPassiveTotal(heroId,"damageReduction",null,true));
  return damageType==="magic"?{score:d.magicResistance,reduction:d.magicResistance+passive,min:r.minMagicDamage,label:"Résistance magique"+(passive?" + passifs":"")}:{score:d.armor,reduction:d.armorReduction+passive,min:r.minPhysicalDamage,label:"Armure"+(passive?" + passifs":"")};
}

--- minPhysicalDamage in dungeonMitigationForEnemy ---
function dungeonMitigationForEnemy(def,damageType){
  const d=dungeonEnemyDerived(def),r=loadDungeonRpgRules();
  return damageType==="magic"?{score:d.magicResistance,reduction:d.magicResistance,min:r.minMagicDamage,label:"Résistance magique"}:{score:d.armor,reduction:d.armorReduction,min:r.minPhysicalDamage,label:"Armure"};
}

function refreshTexts144(root=document){
  root.querySelectorAll(".captureAbilityMini button,.cap130Moves button,.capOverlayMoves134 button").forEach(btn=>{
    const oc=btn.getAttribute("onclick")||"";
    const id=oc.match(/showAbilityInfo\('([^']+)'\)/)?.[1];if(!id)return;
    const a=captureBattleAbilityById(id);if(!a)return;
    const span=btn.querySelector("span,small");if(span)span.textContent=cap144AbilitySummary(a);
    const smalls=btn.querySelectorAll("small");
    if(smalls.length>1)smalls[0].textContent=cap144AbilitySummary(a);
  });
}
const rend144=window.captureRenderBattleLive;
window.captureRenderBattleLive=function(){
  const r=rend144.apply(this,arguments);
  refreshTexts144(document);
  return r;
};

};

  const r=overlay144.apply(this,arguments);setTimeout(()=>refreshTexts144(document),0);return r;
};

    box.style.display="flex";
  },0);
  return r;
};
})();

<script id="gensStability151">
(function(){
"use strict";

/* =========================================================
   GenSrpG V16.151 — consolidation stabilité
   Une seule couche gère :
   - changement Capture / Dungeon
   - wizard de préparation
   - bouton MJ Dungeon
   - réglages MJ Dungeon
   - filtrage visuel des objets
   Aucun polling, aucun MutationObserver permanent.
   ========================================================= */

window.gensMode151=function(){
  try{
    const p=getActiveGameProfile?.();
    const fam=typeof gensCurrentContentFamily==="function"?gensCurrentContentFamily():"";
    if(p?.gameStyle==="dungeon" && fam==="creature")return "capture";
    if(p?.gameStyle==="dungeon")return "dungeon";
    return "other";
  }catch(e){return "other"}
};

window.dungeonMjRules151=function(){
  const d={
    xpMultiplier:1,goldMultiplier:1,lootMultiplier:1,difficulty:2,
    enemyControl:"ai",initiativeMode:"strict",aiTactics:"standard",
    mixedBossMj:true,eventFrequency:2
  };
  try{
    const old=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("gensrpg_dungeon_mj_rules_v145")||"{}");
    Object.assign(d,old||{});
  }catch(e){}
  return d;
};

window.saveDungeonMj151=function(){
  const g=id=>document.getElementById(id),r=dungeonMjRules151();
  r.xpMultiplier=Math.max(0,Number(g("dmj151Xp")?.value)||1);
  r.goldMultiplier=Math.max(0,Number(g("dmj151Gold")?.value)||1);
  r.lootMultiplier=Math.max(0,Number(g("dmj151Loot")?.value)||1);
  r.difficulty=Math.max(1,Math.min(4,Number(g("dmj151Diff")?.value)||2));
  r.enemyControl=g("dmj151Control")?.value||"ai";
  r.initiativeMode=g("dmj151Init")?.value||"strict";
  r.aiTactics=g("dmj151Ai")?.value||"standard";
  r.mixedBossMj=!!g("dmj151Boss")?.checked;
  r.eventFrequency=Math.max(1,Math.min(3,Number(g("dmj151Events")?.value)||2));
  localStorage.setItem("gensrpg_dungeon_mj_rules_v145",JSON.stringify(r));
  const m=document.getElementById("dungeonMj151");
  if(m){m.classList.remove("open");m.style.display="none"}
};

window.openDungeonMj151=function(){
  if(gensMode151()!=="dungeon")return;
  let m=document.getElementById("dungeonMj151");
  if(!m){
    m=document.createElement("div");
    m.id="dungeonMj151";m.className="gensMjOverlay151";
    m.innerHTML=`<div class="gensMjCard151">
      <button class="gensMjClose151" onclick="document.getElementById('dungeonMj151').style.display='none';document.getElementById('dungeonMj151').classList.remove('open')">✕</button>

  set("dmj151Diff",r.difficulty);set("dmj151Control",r.enemyControl);set("dmj151Init",r.initiativeMode);

  m.style.display="flex";m.classList.add("open");

/* API conservée pour les futures fonctions combat Dungeon. */
window.dungeonEnemyController147=function(enemy){
  const r=dungeonMjRules151();
  if(r.enemyControl==="ai"||r.enemyControl==="mj")return r.enemyControl;
  const elite=!!(enemy?.boss||enemy?.isBoss||enemy?.elite||enemy?.isElite||/boss|élite|elite/i.test(String(enemy?.rank||enemy?.type||"")));
  return r.mixedBossMj&&elite?"mj":"ai";
};
window.dungeonChooseAiAction147=function(enemy,heroes,allies,abilities){
  const r=dungeonMjRules151(),alive=(heroes||[]).filter(h=>!h.ko&&(Number(h.hp??h.currentHp)||0)>0);
  const usable=(abilities||[]).filter(a=>!a.disabled);
  if(!alive.length)return null;
  let target=alive[0],ability=usable[0]||null;
  if(r.aiTactics==="basic")return {target,ability};
  if(r.aiTactics==="standard"){
    target=[...alive].sort((a,b)=>(Number(a.hp??a.currentHp)||0)-(Number(b.hp??b.currentHp)||0))[0];
    return {target,ability};
  }
  const hurt=(allies||[]).filter(a=>!a.ko&&Number(a.hp??a.currentHp)<Number(a.maxHp||1)*.4)
    .sort((a,b)=>Number(a.hp??a.currentHp)-Number(b.hp??b.currentHp))[0];
  const heals=usable.filter(a=>/heal|soin|hot/i.test(JSON.stringify(a)));
  if(hurt&&heals.length)return {target:hurt,ability:heals[0],friendly:true};
  const aoe=usable.filter(a=>/zone|aoe|all_enemies|tous les ennemis/i.test(JSON.stringify(a)));
  if(alive.length>=2&&aoe.length)return {target:alive[0],ability:aoe[0],aoe:true};
  target=[...alive].sort((a,b)=>
    ((Number(a.hp??a.currentHp)||0)/(Number(a.maxHp)||1))-
    ((Number(b.hp??b.currentHp)||0)/(Number(b.maxHp)||1))
  )[0];
  return {target,ability};
};
window.dungeonResolveEnemyTurn147=function(enemy,heroes,allies,abilities){
  if(dungeonEnemyController147(enemy)==="mj")return {mode:"pause_mj",enemy};
  return {mode:"ai",...dungeonChooseAiAction147(enemy,heroes,allies,abilities)};
};
window.dungeonBuildTimeline147=function(combatants,currentId){
  const arr=(combatants||[]).filter(x=>!x.ko).map(x=>({
    id:x.id||x.instanceId,
    name:x.heroName?`${x.heroName} → ${x.name||"Créature"}`:(x.name||"Combattant"),
    side:x.side||x.team||"enemy",
    initiative:Number(x.initiative??x.speed??x.agi)||0
  })).sort((a,b)=>b.initiative-a.initiative);
  const i=Math.max(0,arr.findIndex(x=>String(x.id)===String(currentId)));
  return [...arr.slice(i),...arr.slice(0,i)].slice(0,8);
};

/* Filtre visuel, sans modifier/supprimer aucun objet sauvegardé. */
window.gensFilterItems151=function(){
  const mode=gensMode151();
  const allowed=typeof allowedObjectIdsForActiveProfile==="function"?allowedObjectIdsForActiveProfile():null;
  const roots=["itemsList","itemList","itemsEditorList","rpgItemsList"].map(id=>document.getElementById(id)).filter(Boolean);
  roots.forEach(root=>{
    [...root.children].forEach(card=>{
      const oc=card.querySelector("[onclick*='editItem']")?.getAttribute("onclick")||card.getAttribute("onclick")||"";
      const id=oc.match(/editItem\(['"]([^'"]+)/)?.[1];
      if(!id||!allowed){card.style.removeProperty("display");return}
      card.style.display=allowed.has(String(id))?"":"none";
    });
  });
};

/* Bouton MJ Dungeon : 1 seul, dans l'étape visible. */
window.ensureDungeonMj151=function(){
  ["dungeonMjBtn145","dungeonMjMain149","dungeonMjMain150"].forEach(id=>document.getElementById(id)?.remove());
  let b=document.getElementById("dungeonMjMain151");
  if(gensMode151()!=="dungeon"){b?.remove();return}

  const profile=document.getElementById("pregameProfileStep");
  const heroes=document.getElementById("pregameHeroStep");
  let host=null;
  if(profile&&getComputedStyle(profile).display!=="none")host=profile;
  else if(heroes&&getComputedStyle(heroes).display!=="none")host=heroes;
  if(!host)return;

  if(!b){
    b=document.createElement("button");b.id="dungeonMjMain151";b.type="button";b.className="dungeonMjMain151";
    b.innerHTML="<span>🎛️</span><div><b>RÉGLAGES MJ — DUNGEON</b><small>XP · Or · difficulté · butin · événements · IA / contrôle des ennemis</small></div>";
    b.onclick=openDungeonMj151;
  }
  if(b.parentElement!==host){
    b.remove();
    const cont=host.querySelector(".pregameContinue");
    if(cont)host.insertBefore(b,cont);else host.prepend(b);
  }
};

/* En jeu Dungeon : accès aux règles MJ sans remplacer le panneau de scène existant. */

/* Réconciliation unique de l'interface. */
window.gensReconcile151=function(reason=""){
  const mode=gensMode151();

  /* Le wizard natif reste la seule source de vérité pour ses étapes. */
  try{if(typeof updatePregameWizard==="function")updatePregameWizard()}catch(e){}

  const profile=document.getElementById("pregameProfileStep");
  const heroes=document.getElementById("pregameHeroStep");
  if(profile&&heroes){
    const pVisible=getComputedStyle(profile).display!=="none";
    const hVisible=getComputedStyle(heroes).display!=="none";
    if(pVisible&&hVisible){
      /* sécurité contre les anciennes fuites d'affichage */
      if(typeof gensPregameStep!=="undefined"&&gensPregameStep==="heroes")profile.style.display="none";
      else heroes.style.display="none";
    }
  }

  const capHub=document.getElementById("captureGameHub");
  const dunPanel=document.getElementById("dungeonMenuPanel");
  if(mode!=="capture"&&capHub)capHub.style.display="none";
  if(mode!=="dungeon"&&dunPanel)dunPanel.style.display="none";

  /* Les anciens boutons MJ Capture n'apparaissent jamais en Dungeon. */
  if(mode==="dungeon"){
    ["captureMjBig137","captureMjInGame137","preMj130Btn"].forEach(id=>{
      const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.style.display="none";
    });
  }

  try{gensEnsureDungeonAdventureButton139?.()}catch(e){}
  try{gensFilterItems151()}catch(e){}
  ensureDungeonMj151();
  ensureDungeonInGameMj151();

  if(mode==="capture"){
    try{renderCaptureWorldHub?.()}catch(e){}
  }else if(mode==="dungeon"){
    try{updateDungeonExploreButtons?.()}catch(e){}
  }
};

/* Changement de profil : nettoyage synchrone, puis un seul rerender. */
const apply151=window.applyGameProfile;
if(typeof apply151==="function"){
  window.applyGameProfile=function(){
    const before=gensMode151();
    const r=apply151.apply(this,arguments);
    const after=gensMode151();

    /* on revient toujours à l'étape MODE après un vrai changement de profil,
       sans afficher déjà l'étape Réglages. */
    try{
      if(before!==after && !hasActiveSession?.()){
        gensPregameStep="profile";
      }
    }catch(e){}

    requestAnimationFrame(()=>gensReconcile151("profile"));
    return r;
  };
}

/* Navigation wizard : réconciliation après l'action, pas d'observer/polling. */
["showPregameProfileStep","showPregameHeroStep","restorePregameWizard","backHome"].forEach(name=>{
  const old=window[name];if(typeof old!=="function")return;
  window[name]=function(){
    const r=old.apply(this,arguments);
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>gensReconcile151(name));
    return r;
  };
});

}

})();

<script id="forceReload155">
(function(){
"use strict";

/* V16.155 — compare la VRAIE famille de contenu :
   survival / rpg / creature / manga.
   Capture et Dungeon étaient tous deux "adventure", ce qui empêchait les V154 et précédentes de déclencher le reload. */

window.gensProfileContentFamily155=function(profileId){
  try{
    const p=loadGameProfiles().find(x=>String(x.id)===String(profileId));
    return p?gensContentFamilyForProfile(p):"unknown";
  }catch(e){return"unknown"}
};

window.gensSwitchUniverse155=function(profileId,family){
  const previousId=activeGameProfileId();
  const from=gensProfileContentFamily155(previousId);
  const to=gensProfileContentFamily155(profileId);

  /* même univers ou même famille : fonctionnement normal */
  if(!previousId || String(previousId)===String(profileId) || from===to){
    return window._openGensBuiltInGame155(profileId,family);
  }

  /* changement réel de moteur/famille : aucune construction du nouveau DOM */
  if(hasActiveSession?.()){
    const target=loadGameProfiles().find(x=>String(x.id)===String(profileId));
    const ok=confirm(
      "Changer de mode vers « "+(target?.name||"ce mode")+" » ?\n\n"+
      "La session active sera quittée puis GenSrpG redémarrera pour éviter les mélanges d’interface."
    );
    if(!ok)return;
    try{if(current&&state)save()}catch(e){}
    try{closeTurnPopup()}catch(e){}
    try{markSessionActive(false)}catch(e){}
  }

  try{
    setActiveGameProfileId(profileId);
    localStorage.setItem("gensrpg_forced_mode_reload_155",String(profileId));
  }catch(e){}

  /* petit écran visible juste avant le reload */
  let ov=document.getElementById("gensForceReload155");
  if(!ov){
    ov=document.createElement("div");
    ov.id="gensForceReload155";
    ov.innerHTML="<div><div>🔄</div><b>CHANGEMENT DE MODE</b><span>Rechargement de GenSrpG…</span></div>";
    document.body.appendChild(ov);
  }
  ov.style.display="flex";

  /* laisse le navigateur peindre l'overlay puis recharge tout */
  setTimeout(()=>location.reload(),120);
};

/* Capture la VRAIE fonction appelée par les cartes univers. */
window._openGensBuiltInGame155=window.openGensBuiltInGame;
window.openGensBuiltInGame=function(profileId,family){
  return gensSwitchUniverse155(profileId,family);
};

/* Au boot, le profil forcé reste la source de vérité avant le premier rendu. */
try{
  const forced=localStorage.getItem("gensrpg_forced_mode_reload_155");
  if(forced){
    setActiveGameProfileId(forced);
    localStorage.removeItem("gensrpg_forced_mode_reload_155");
  }
}catch(e){}
})();
</script>

<script id="coreCombatPoolFix156">
(function(){
"use strict";

/* =========================================================
   V16.156
   1) Zombicide 40K base respecte son heroPool enregistré.
   2) Dungeon : timeline + IA/MJ réellement branchés au combat.
   3) Capture MJ : ciblage manuel des créatures du joueur.
   ========================================================= */

/* ---------- 1. POOL HÉROS ZOMBICIDE BASE ---------- */
const oldAllowedHeroes156=window.allowedHeroIdsForActiveProfile;
window.allowedHeroIdsForActiveProfile=function(){
  const id=activeGameProfileId();
  if(String(id)===String(GAME_PROFILE_BASE_ID)){
    const p=activeGameProfileRaw?.() || loadGameProfilesRaw().find(x=>String(x?.id)===String(id));
    if(p?.modules?.heroes===false)return new Set();
    if(Array.isArray(p?.heroPool)){
      return new Set(
        p.heroPool.map(String).filter(hid=>{
          const custom=findCustomHero?.(hid);
          const dungeon=(typeof DUNGEON_HERO_IDS!=="undefined"&&DUNGEON_HERO_IDS.includes(String(hid))) ||
            (custom&&typeof isDungeonHeroRecord==="function"&&isDungeonHeroRecord(custom));
          return !dungeon;
        })
      );
    }
  }
  return oldAllowedHeroes156.apply(this,arguments);
};

/* disponibilité participants = intersection stricte avec le pool */
const oldAvail156=window.availableParticipantHeroIds;
if(typeof oldAvail156==="function"){
  window.availableParticipantHeroIds=function(){
    const ids=oldAvail156.apply(this,arguments)||[];
    const allowed=allowedHeroIdsForActiveProfile();
    if(!allowed)return ids;
    return ids.filter(id=>allowed.has(String(id)));
  };
}

/* purge une ancienne sélection devenue interdite */
window.gensNormalizeParticipants156=function(){
  try{
    const allowed=allowedHeroIdsForActiveProfile();
    if(!allowed)return;
    const currentIds=loadGameParticipants().map(String);
    const clean=currentIds.filter(id=>allowed.has(String(id)));
    if(clean.length!==currentIds.length)saveGameParticipants(clean);
  }catch(e){}
};
const oldRenderParticipants156=window.renderParticipantSelector;
window.renderParticipantSelector=function(){
  gensNormalizeParticipants156();
  return oldRenderParticipants156.apply(this,arguments);
};


/* ---------- 2. DUNGEON : VRAI TOUR / TIMELINE ---------- */
window.dungeonTurn156={active:false,order:[],index:0,aiBusy:false,lastSig:""};

window.dungeonBuildOrder156=function(){
  const order=[];
  const heroes=(dungeonCombatSelection?.heroes||[]).map(id=>{
    const snap=dungeonCombatHeroSnapshot(id);
    return {kind:"hero",id:String(id),name:CHARS[id]?.name||id,initiative:Number(snap?.initiative)||0};
  });
  const enemies=loadActiveEnemies()
    .filter(e=>(dungeonCombatSelection?.enemies||[]).includes(e.id)&&!e.defeated&&Number(e.hp)>0)
    .map(e=>{
      const d=activeEnemyDefinition(e.enemyId),ed=dungeonEnemyDerivedForInstance(e)||dungeonEnemyDerived(d);
      return {kind:"enemy",id:String(e.id),name:d?.name||"Ennemi",initiative:Number(ed?.initiative)||0};
    });
  order.push(...heroes,...enemies);
  order.sort((a,b)=>b.initiative-a.initiative || (a.kind==="hero"?-1:1));
  return order;
};

window.dungeonInitTurn156=function(){
  dungeonTurn156.active=true;
  dungeonTurn156.order=dungeonBuildOrder156();
  dungeonTurn156.index=0;
  dungeonTurn156.aiBusy=false;
  dungeonTurn156.lastSig="";
};

window.dungeonCurrentTurn156=function(){
  if(!dungeonTurn156.active)return null;
  const living=dungeonBuildOrder156();
  const alive=new Set(living.map(x=>x.kind+":"+x.id));
  dungeonTurn156.order=dungeonTurn156.order.filter(x=>alive.has(x.kind+":"+x.id));
  living.forEach(x=>{
    if(!dungeonTurn156.order.some(y=>y.kind===x.kind&&y.id===x.id))dungeonTurn156.order.push(x);
  });
  if(!dungeonTurn156.order.length)return null;
  if(dungeonTurn156.index>=dungeonTurn156.order.length)dungeonTurn156.index=0;
  return dungeonTurn156.order[dungeonTurn156.index];
};

window.dungeonAdvanceTurn156=function(){
  if(!dungeonTurn156.active)return;
  const order=dungeonBuildOrder156();
  if(!order.length)return;
  const current=dungeonCurrentTurn156();
  dungeonTurn156.order=order;
  let idx=current?order.findIndex(x=>x.kind===current.kind&&x.id===current.id):-1;
  dungeonTurn156.index=(idx<0?0:(idx+1)%order.length);
  dungeonTurn156.aiBusy=false;
  setTimeout(()=>{try{renderDungeonCombatRound()}catch(e){}},60);
};

window.dungeonTimelineHtml156=function(){
  const order=dungeonTurn156.order||[];
  const cur=dungeonCurrentTurn156();
  if(!order.length||!cur)return "";
  const idx=order.findIndex(x=>x.kind===cur.kind&&x.id===cur.id);
  const rotated=[...order.slice(idx),...order.slice(0,idx)].slice(0,8);
  return `<div class="dungeonTimeline156">${rotated.map((x,i)=>`
    <div class="dungeonTimelineNode156 ${x.kind} ${i===0?"now":""}">
      <small>${i===0?"MAINTENANT":"PUIS"}</small>
      <b>${z40kEscHtml(x.name)}</b>
      <span>⚡ ${x.initiative}</span>
    </div>`).join("")}</div>`;
};

window.dungeonAiTarget156=function(){
  const rules=dungeonMjRules151?.()||{};
  const heroes=(dungeonCombatSelection?.heroes||[]).map(id=>({id,snap:dungeonCombatHeroSnapshot(id)}))
    .filter(x=>Number(x.snap?.hp)>0);
  if(!heroes.length)return null;
  if(rules.aiTactics==="basic")return heroes[Math.floor(Math.random()*heroes.length)].id;
  if(rules.aiTactics==="advanced"){
    return [...heroes].sort((a,b)=>
      (Number(a.snap.hp)/Math.max(1,Number(a.snap.maxHp)))-
      (Number(b.snap.hp)/Math.max(1,Number(b.snap.maxHp)))
    )[0].id;
  }
  return [...heroes].sort((a,b)=>Number(a.snap.hp)-Number(b.snap.hp))[0].id;
};

window.dungeonRunAi156=function(actor){
  if(!actor||actor.kind!=="enemy"||dungeonTurn156.aiBusy)return;
  const inst=loadActiveEnemies().find(e=>String(e.id)===String(actor.id));
  if(!inst)return dungeonAdvanceTurn156();
  const def=activeEnemyDefinition(inst.enemyId);
  const skills=dungeonEnemyAttackSkills(def);
  if(!skills.length)return dungeonAdvanceTurn156();
  dungeonTurn156.aiBusy=true;
  const rules=dungeonMjRules151?.()||{};
  let skill=skills[0];
  if(rules.aiTactics==="advanced"){
    skill=[...skills].sort((a,b)=>(Number(b.power||b.damage||0))-(Number(a.power||a.damage||0)))[0]||skills[0];
  }else if(rules.aiTactics==="standard"){
    skill=skills[Math.floor(Math.random()*skills.length)]||skills[0];
  }
  const target=dungeonAiTarget156();
  setTimeout(()=>{
    try{rollDungeonEnemyAttackSkill(inst.id,skill.id,target)}catch(e){console.warn(e)}
    setTimeout(()=>dungeonAdvanceTurn156(),1400);
  },450);
};

window.dungeonShouldMjControl156=function(actor){
  const rules=dungeonMjRules151?.()||{};
  if(rules.enemyControl==="mj")return true;
  if(rules.enemyControl==="ai")return false;
  if(rules.enemyControl==="mixed"){
    const inst=loadActiveEnemies().find(e=>String(e.id)===String(actor?.id));
    const def=inst?activeEnemyDefinition(inst.enemyId):null;
    const elite=!!(def?.boss||def?.isBoss||def?.elite||def?.isElite||/boss|élite|elite/i.test(String(def?.rank||def?.type||"")));
    return !!rules.mixedBossMj&&elite;
  }
  return false;
};

/* start combat initialise réellement la timeline */
const oldStartDungeon156=window.startDungeonClassicCombat;
window.startDungeonClassicCombat=function(){
  const r=oldStartDungeon156.apply(this,arguments);
  dungeonInitTurn156();
  setTimeout(()=>renderDungeonCombatRound(),0);
  return r;
};

/* clic héros = consomme réellement son tour */
["dungeonCombatAttack","dungeonCombatBasicAttack","dungeonCombatUseActiveTalent"].forEach(name=>{
  const old=window[name];
  if(typeof old!=="function")return;
  window[name]=function(heroId){
    const cur=dungeonCurrentTurn156();
    const strict=(dungeonMjRules151?.().initiativeMode||"strict")==="strict";
    if(strict && cur && !(cur.kind==="hero"&&String(cur.id)===String(heroId))){
      alert("Ce n'est pas le tour de ce héros.");
      return;
    }
    const r=old.apply(this,arguments);
    if(cur?.kind==="hero")setTimeout(()=>dungeonAdvanceTurn156(),250);
    return r;
  };
});

/* clic ennemi MJ = consomme son tour */
const oldEnemySkill156=window.rollDungeonEnemyAttackSkill;
window.rollDungeonEnemyAttackSkill=function(instanceId,skillId,targetHeroId=null){
  const cur=dungeonCurrentTurn156();
  const rules=dungeonMjRules151?.()||{};
  const strict=rules.initiativeMode==="strict";
  if(dungeonTurn156.active && strict && cur && !(cur.kind==="enemy"&&String(cur.id)===String(instanceId))){
    alert("Ce n'est pas le tour de cet ennemi.");
    return;
  }
  const r=oldEnemySkill156.apply(this,arguments);
  if(dungeonTurn156.active && dungeonShouldMjControl156(cur)){
    setTimeout(()=>dungeonAdvanceTurn156(),300);
  }
  return r;
};

/* post-traitement Dungeon 156 retiré en Core 3.03.
   Les helpers initiative/IA 156 restent définis pour compatibilité avec les anciennes fonctions,
   mais 156 ne dessine plus de timeline, ne verrouille plus les cartes et ne lance plus l'IA.
   L'autorité visuelle et de tour est exclusivement Core 3.02+. */

/* ---------- 3. CAPTURE MJ : CIBLE JOUEUR MANUELLE ---------- */
window.captureMjPlayerTarget156=null;

window.captureSelectMjPlayerTarget156=function(instanceId){
  const b=window.gensCurrentCaptureBattle;if(!b)return;
  const h=gensCaptureBattleCreature(b,instanceId);
  if(!h||h.sideKey!=="A"||h.creature.ko)return;
  captureMjPlayerTarget156=instanceId;
  b._mjPlayerTarget156=instanceId;
  captureRenderBattleLive(b);
};

/* remplace uniquement l'action MJ ennemie */
window.captureMjEnemyUseAbility137=function(abilityId){
  const b=window.gensCurrentCaptureBattle;if(!b||b._actionLocked137)return;
  let actor=captureBattleCurrentActor(b);
  const rules=captureMjRules137();
  if(rules.enemyControl!=="mj"||!actor||actor.sideKey!=="B")return;

  if(!rules.respectTimeline && b._mjEnemyActorId137){
    const h=gensCaptureBattleCreature(b,b._mjEnemyActorId137);
    if(h&&h.sideKey==="B"&&!h.creature.ko)actor={...actor,...h};
  }

  const ab=captureBattleAbilityById(abilityId)||captureBattleAbilities(actor.creature).find(x=>x.id===abilityId);
  if(!ab)return;

  let target=null;
  const wanted=b._mjPlayerTarget156||captureMjPlayerTarget156;
  if(wanted){
    const h=gensCaptureBattleCreature(b,wanted);
    if(h&&h.sideKey==="A"&&!h.creature.ko)target=h.creature;
  }
  if(!target)target=gensCaptureBattleActiveCreatures(b,"A").find(c=>!c.ko)||null;
  if(!target)return;

  b._actionLocked137=true;
  const result=captureBattleApplyAbility(b,actor.creature,ab,target);
  captureRenderBattleLive(b);
  try{if(result?.ok)cap143FxForAbility?.(b,ab,target,result,"B")}catch(e){}
  setTimeout(()=>{
    gensCaptureBattleEvaluateVictory(b);
    if(b.phase!=="ended"){gensCaptureBattleEndTurn(b);gensCaptureBattleStartTurn(b)}
    b._actionLocked137=false;
    b._mjPlayerTarget156=null;captureMjPlayerTarget156=null;
    captureRenderBattleLive(b);
  },800);
};

/* dernier wrapper de rendu : clic sur une créature joueur = cible MJ,
   seulement pendant le tour d'un ennemi contrôlé par le MJ */
const oldCaptureRender156=window.captureRenderBattleLive;
window.captureRenderBattleLive=function(b=window.gensCurrentCaptureBattle){
  const r=oldCaptureRender156.apply(this,arguments);
  const host=document.getElementById("captureBattleLiveBody");
  if(!host||!b)return r;
  const rules=captureMjRules137?.()||{};
  const actor=captureBattleCurrentActor(b);
  const mjEnemy=rules.enemyControl==="mj"&&actor?.sideKey==="B"&&b.phase!=="ended";

  if(mjEnemy){
    host.querySelectorAll(".captureFightSide:not(.enemy) .captureFightMon, .captureFightSide.player .captureFightMon").forEach(card=>{
      const name=card.querySelector("strong")?.textContent||"";
      const c=(b.sides?.A?.creatures||[]).find(x=>x.name===name);
      if(!c||c.ko)return;
      card.onclick=()=>captureSelectMjPlayerTarget156(c.instanceId);
      card.style.cursor="crosshair";
      card.classList.toggle("capMjPlayerTarget156",String(b._mjPlayerTarget156||captureMjPlayerTarget156)===String(c.instanceId));
    });

    let hint=host.querySelector(".capMjTargetHint156");
    if(!hint){
      hint=document.createElement("div");hint.className="capMjTargetHint156";
      const action=host.querySelector(".captureFightActions");
      action?.prepend(hint);
    }
    if(hint){
      const h=(b.sides?.A?.creatures||[]).find(x=>String(x.instanceId)===String(b._mjPlayerTarget156||captureMjPlayerTarget156));
      hint.textContent=h?`🎯 Cible MJ : ${h.name}`:"🎯 MJ : clique sur une créature du joueur pour la cibler";
    }
  }
  return r;
};

})();
</script>

<script id="rpgTurnTargetFlow157" type="application/x-gensrpg-disabled">
(function(){
"use strict";

/* =========================================================
   V16.157
   - Dungeon MJ : clic direct sur le héros cible.
   - Timeline Dungeon simplifiée.
   - Tour de partie : popup immédiat + ouverture directe
     de la fiche du joueur actif au démarrage et au joueur suivant.
   ========================================================= */

/* ---------- TOUR GLOBAL : démarrage immédiat ---------- */
window.startTurnManagerForGame=function(){
  const cfg=loadGameCustomization();
  saveTurnState(null);

  if(!cfg.turnOrder){
    renderTurnUi();
    return false;
  }

  applyCustomHeroesMulti();
  const heroes=turnHeroOrderForRound(1);
  if(!heroes.length){
    renderTurnUi();
    return false;
  }

  const st={round:1,phase:"hero",heroes:[...heroes],index:0,startedAt:Date.now()};
  saveTurnState(st);
  gensDismissedTurnPopupSignature="";
  renderTurnUi();

  /* On ouvre D'ABORD la fiche (openChar ferme les popups),
     puis on affiche le popup dans la même frame. */
  const first=heroes[0];
  if(first){
    try{openChar(first)}catch(e){console.warn("Ouverture joueur actif",e)}
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      renderTurnUi();
      showTurnPopup(true);
    });
  }else{
    showTurnPopup(true);
  }
  return true;
};

/* Passage au joueur suivant sans retour parasite au hub. */
window.finishCurrentHeroTurn=function(){
  const cfg=loadGameCustomization();
  let st=loadTurnState();

  if(!cfg.turnOrder){
    alert("La gestion des tours est désactivée.");
    return;
  }
  if(!st || !Array.isArray(st.heroes) || !st.heroes.length){
    st=createTurnState();
    saveTurnState(st);
  }

  const active=st.phase==="hero"?String(st.heroes[st.index]||""):"";
  if(!active){
    renderTurnUi();showTurnPopup(true);return;
  }
  if(String(current||"")!==active){
    alert("Ce n’est pas le tour de "+(CHARS[active]?.name||findCustomHero(active)?.name||active)+".");
    return;
  }

  /* joueur suivant */
  if(st.index<st.heroes.length-1){
    st.index=Number(st.index||0)+1;
    saveTurnState(st);
    gensDismissedTurnPopupSignature="";
    const next=String(st.heroes[st.index]||"");
    if(next){
      try{openChar(next)}catch(e){}
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>{renderTurnUi();showTurnPopup(true)});
    }
    return;
  }

  /* phase intermédiaire éventuelle */
  if(cfg.intermediate){
    st.phase="intermediate";
    saveTurnState(st);
    gensDismissedTurnPopupSignature="";
    try{goMenu()}catch(e){}
    renderTurnUi();
    closeTurnPopup();
    return;
  }

  /* nouveau tour */
  const previousRound=Number(st.round)||1;
  const dungeonEvent=isDungeonMode()?triggerDungeonTurnEvent(previousRound):null;
  st.round=previousRound+1;
  tickAllTurnCooldowns();
  tickDungeonTalentRuntimeEffects();
  applyDungeonHeroStatusRoundEffects();
  applyDungeonPassiveRoundEffects();
  st.index=0;
  st.heroes=turnHeroOrderForRound(st.round);
  st.phase="hero";
  saveTurnState(st);
  gensDismissedTurnPopupSignature="";

  const next=st.heroes[0];
  if(next){
    try{openChar(next)}catch(e){}
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      renderTurnUi();
      if(dungeonEvent){
        showEffectPopup("🔄","NOUVEAU TOUR","<strong>Tour "+st.round+"</strong>","Tour "+previousRound+" terminé.",true);
        setTimeout(()=>showTurnPopup(true),350);
      }else showTurnPopup(true);
    });
  }
};

/* Popup plus court/clair en Dungeon */
const oldShowTurn157=window.showTurnPopup;
window.showTurnPopup=function(share=false){
  oldShowTurn157(share);
  if(!isDungeonMode())return;
  const st=loadTurnState(),id=currentTurnHeroId();
  if(st?.phase==="hero"&&id){
    const hint=document.getElementById("turnModalHint");
    if(hint)hint.textContent="Joue "+(CHARS[id]?.name||id)+", puis termine son tour depuis sa fiche.";
    const btn=document.getElementById("turnModalOpenHero");
    if(btn)btn.style.display="none"; /* la fiche est déjà ouverte */
  }
};


/* ---------- DUNGEON MJ : CIBLAGE DIRECT DES HÉROS ---------- */
window.dungeonMjHeroTarget157=null;

window.dungeonSelectMjHeroTarget157=function(heroId){
  const snap=dungeonCombatHeroSnapshot(heroId);
  if(!snap||Number(snap.hp)<=0)return;
  dungeonMjHeroTarget157=String(heroId);
  dungeonCombatEnemyTargetHeroId=String(heroId);
  renderDungeonCombatRound();
};

/* Le clic d'une attaque MJ utilise la cible sélectionnée. */
const enemySkill157=window.rollDungeonEnemyAttackSkill;
window.rollDungeonEnemyAttackSkill=function(instanceId,skillId,targetHeroId=null){
  const rules=dungeonMjRules151?.()||{};
  const cur=typeof dungeonCurrentTurn156==="function"?dungeonCurrentTurn156():null;
  const mjTurn=cur?.kind==="enemy" && typeof dungeonShouldMjControl156==="function" && dungeonShouldMjControl156(cur);

  let target=targetHeroId;
  if(mjTurn && dungeonMjHeroTarget157){
    target=dungeonMjHeroTarget157;
  }
  return enemySkill157(instanceId,skillId,target);
};

/* Timeline plus compacte : uniquement acteur actuel + 4 suivants */
window.dungeonTimelineHtml156=function(){
  const order=dungeonTurn156?.order||[];
  const cur=dungeonCurrentTurn156?.();
  if(!order.length||!cur)return "";
  const idx=order.findIndex(x=>x.kind===cur.kind&&x.id===cur.id);
  const rotated=[...order.slice(idx),...order.slice(0,idx)].slice(0,5);
  return `<div class="dungeonTimelineTitle157">⏱️ ORDRE D'INITIATIVE</div>
    <div class="dungeonTimeline156">${rotated.map((x,i)=>`
      <div class="dungeonTimelineNode156 ${x.kind} ${i===0?"now":""}">
        <small>${i===0?"JOUE":"→"}</small>
        <b>${z40kEscHtml(x.name)}</b>
        <span>${x.initiative}</span>
      </div>`).join("")}</div>`;
};

/* Post-rendu Dungeon : cartes héros cliquables pendant un tour MJ ennemi. */
const renderDungeon157=window.renderDungeonCombatRound;
window.renderDungeonCombatRound=function(){
  const r=renderDungeon157.apply(this,arguments);
  if(!dungeonCombatActive)return r;

  const cur=dungeonCurrentTurn156?.();
  const mjEnemy=cur?.kind==="enemy" && dungeonShouldMjControl156?.(cur);
  const host=document.getElementById("dungeonCombatSetupView");
  if(!host)return r;

  /* cache le gros statut de tour global dans le combat : la timeline combat suffit */
  ["turnStatusBar","sheetTurnStatusBar"].forEach(id=>{
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el && dungeonCombatActive)el.style.display="none";
  });

  const heroIds=[...(dungeonCombatSelection?.heroes||[])]
    .sort((a,b)=>dungeonCombatHeroSnapshot(b).initiative-dungeonCombatHeroSnapshot(a).initiative);

  document.querySelectorAll("#combatHeroSide .combatantCard").forEach((card,i)=>{
    const id=String(heroIds[i]||"");
    if(!id)return;

    card.classList.toggle("dungeonMjTarget157",mjEnemy && String(dungeonMjHeroTarget157)===id);

    if(mjEnemy){
      card.style.cursor="crosshair";
      card.onclick=(ev)=>{
        /* stats reste utilisable sans modifier la cible */
        if(ev.target?.closest?.(".dungeonCombatStatsBtn"))return;
        dungeonSelectMjHeroTarget157(id);
      };
    }else{
      card.style.cursor="";
      card.onclick=null;
    }
  });

  let targetBox=host.querySelector(".dungeonMjTargetBox157");
  if(mjEnemy){
    if(!targetBox){
      targetBox=document.createElement("div");
      targetBox.className="dungeonMjTargetBox157";
      const tl=document.getElementById("dungeonTimeline156");
      if(tl)tl.insertAdjacentElement("afterend",targetBox);
      else host.prepend(targetBox);
    }
    const name=dungeonMjHeroTarget157?(CHARS[dungeonMjHeroTarget157]?.name||dungeonMjHeroTarget157):"";
    targetBox.innerHTML=name
      ? `🎯 Cible du MJ : <b>${z40kEscHtml(name)}</b>`
      : `🎯 MJ : clique sur un héros pour choisir la cible`;
  }else{
    targetBox?.remove();
    dungeonMjHeroTarget157=null;
  }

  /* Le select historique reste synchronisé mais visuellement secondaire. */
  if(mjEnemy && dungeonMjHeroTarget157){
    document.querySelectorAll("#combatEnemySide .dungeonCombatTargetSelect").forEach(sel=>{
      if([...sel.options].some(o=>String(o.value)===String(dungeonMjHeroTarget157)))
        sel.value=dungeonMjHeroTarget157;
    });
  }
  return r;
};

})();
</script>
