<script id="rpgPlayerTargeting158" type="application/x-gensrpg-disabled">
(function(){
"use strict";

/* V16.158 — ciblage joueur -> ennemi en Dungeon RPG
   Objectif : même logique visuelle que le ciblage MJ, pour préparer
   tanking, provocation, garde, interception, protection, etc. */

window.dungeonPlayerEnemyTarget158=null;

window.dungeonSelectEnemyTarget158=function(enemyId){
  const e=loadActiveEnemies().find(x=>String(x.id)===String(enemyId));
  if(!e||e.defeated||Number(e.hp)<=0)return;
  dungeonPlayerEnemyTarget158=String(enemyId);
  renderDungeonCombatRound();
};

/* Rend la cible joueur exploitable par les actions existantes */
window.dungeonResolvePlayerTarget158=function(fallback){
  const chosen=dungeonPlayerEnemyTarget158;
  if(chosen){
    const e=loadActiveEnemies().find(x=>String(x.id)===String(chosen));
    if(e&&!e.defeated&&Number(e.hp)>0)return String(chosen);
  }
  return fallback;
};

/* Intercepte les attaques héros pour préférer la cible sélectionnée visuellement. */
const atk158=window.dungeonCombatAttack;
if(typeof atk158==="function")window.dungeonCombatAttack=function(heroId,weaponIdx,targetEnemyId){
  return atk158.call(this,heroId,weaponIdx,dungeonResolvePlayerTarget158(targetEnemyId));
};

const basic158=window.dungeonCombatBasicAttack;
if(typeof basic158==="function")window.dungeonCombatBasicAttack=function(heroId,targetEnemyId){
  return basic158.call(this,heroId,dungeonResolvePlayerTarget158(targetEnemyId));
};

const talent158=window.dungeonCombatUseActiveTalent;
if(typeof talent158==="function")window.dungeonCombatUseActiveTalent=function(heroId,talentId,targetId){
  /* Les talents offensifs utilisent la cible ennemie sélectionnée.
     Les talents alliés gardent leur propre targetId. */
  try{
    const t=dungeonUnlockedActiveTalents(heroId).find(x=>String(x.id)===String(talentId));
    const fx=t?.effects||[];
    const needsEnemy=fx.some(dungeonTalentEffectNeedsEnemy);
    const needsAlly=fx.some(dungeonTalentEffectNeedsAlly);
    if(needsEnemy&&!needsAlly)targetId=dungeonResolvePlayerTarget158(targetId);
  }catch(e){}
  return talent158.call(this,heroId,talentId,targetId);
};

/* Rendu : cliquer sur une carte ennemie la cible pour l'équipe joueur. */
const render158=window.renderDungeonCombatRound;
window.renderDungeonCombatRound=function(){
  const r=render158.apply(this,arguments);
  if(!dungeonCombatActive)return r;

  const cur=typeof dungeonCurrentTurn156==="function"?dungeonCurrentTurn156():null;
  const playerTurn=cur?.kind==="hero";
  const host=document.getElementById("dungeonCombatSetupView");
  if(!host)return r;

  const enemies=loadActiveEnemies()
    .filter(e=>(dungeonCombatSelection?.enemies||[]).includes(e.id)&&!e.defeated&&Number(e.hp)>0)
    .sort((a,b)=>(dungeonEnemyDerivedForInstance(b)?.initiative||0)-(dungeonEnemyDerivedForInstance(a)?.initiative||0));

  document.querySelectorAll("#combatEnemySide .combatantCard").forEach((card,i)=>{
    const e=enemies[i];if(!e)return;
    const id=String(e.id);

    card.classList.toggle("dungeonPlayerTarget158",playerTurn&&String(dungeonPlayerEnemyTarget158)===id);

    if(playerTurn){
      card.style.cursor="crosshair";
      card.onclick=(ev)=>{
        if(ev.target?.closest?.(".dungeonCombatStatsBtn"))return;
        dungeonSelectEnemyTarget158(id);
      };
    }

    /* synchronise tous les selects de cible héros */
    if(playerTurn&&dungeonPlayerEnemyTarget158){
      document.querySelectorAll("#combatHeroSide .dungeonCombatTargetSelect").forEach(sel=>{
        if([...sel.options].some(o=>String(o.value)===String(dungeonPlayerEnemyTarget158)))
          sel.value=dungeonPlayerEnemyTarget158;
      });
    }
  });

  let box=host.querySelector(".dungeonPlayerTargetBox158");
  if(playerTurn){
    if(!box){
      box=document.createElement("div");
      box.className="dungeonPlayerTargetBox158";
      const tl=document.getElementById("dungeonTimeline156");
      if(tl)tl.insertAdjacentElement("afterend",box);
      else host.prepend(box);
    }
    const e=enemies.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(dungeonPlayerEnemyTarget158));
    const d=e?activeEnemyDefinition(e.enemyId):null;
    box.innerHTML=e
      ? `🎯 Cible de l'équipe : <b>${z40kEscHtml(d?.name||"Ennemi")}</b> · ❤️ ${Number(e.hp)||0}/${Number(e.maxHp)||0}`
      : `🎯 Clique sur un ennemi pour le cibler`;
  }else{
    box?.remove();
  }

  /* si la cible meurt, on nettoie */
  if(dungeonPlayerEnemyTarget158){
    const alive=enemies.some(e=>String(e.id)===String(dungeonPlayerEnemyTarget158));
    if(!alive)dungeonPlayerEnemyTarget158=null;
  }

  return r;
};

})();
</script>
<style id="rpgPlayerTargeting158Css">
.dungeonPlayerTarget158{
  outline:3px solid #57b8e8!important;
  box-shadow:0 0 16px #57b8e866!important
}
.dungeonPlayerTargetBox158{
  margin:3px 0 10px;
  padding:8px 10px;
  border:1px solid #3f7890;
  border-radius:8px;
  background:#12232b;
  font-size:11px;
  font-weight:800
}
</style>


<script id="rpgHeroTargeting159" type="application/x-gensrpg-disabled">
(function(){
"use strict";

/* V16.159 — correction du ciblage RPG :
   ce n'est PAS une cible globale d'équipe.
   Chaque héros actif choisit sa propre cible ennemie en cliquant sur sa carte.
   Le vieux menu déroulant reste seulement comme fallback interne et est masqué visuellement. */

window.dungeonHeroTargets159 = window.dungeonHeroTargets159 || {};

window.dungeonActiveHero159=function(){
  try{
    const cur=dungeonCurrentTurn156?.();
    if(cur?.kind==="hero")return String(cur.id);
  }catch(e){}
  return null;
};

window.dungeonSelectHeroEnemyTarget159=function(heroId,enemyId){
  heroId=String(heroId||"");
  enemyId=String(enemyId||"");
  if(!heroId||!enemyId)return;
  const e=loadActiveEnemies().find(x=>String(x.id)===enemyId);
  if(!e||e.defeated||Number(e.hp)<=0)return;
  dungeonHeroTargets159[heroId]=enemyId;
  renderDungeonCombatRound();
};

window.dungeonHeroTarget159=function(heroId,fallback=null){
  heroId=String(heroId||"");
  const chosen=dungeonHeroTargets159[heroId];
  if(chosen){
    const e=loadActiveEnemies().find(x=>String(x.id)===String(chosen));
    if(e&&!e.defeated&&Number(e.hp)>0)return String(chosen);
    delete dungeonHeroTargets159[heroId];
  }
  return fallback;
};

/* remplace le ciblage global V158 par un ciblage par héros */
const atk159=window.dungeonCombatAttack;
window.dungeonCombatAttack=function(heroId,weaponIdx,targetEnemyId){
  return atk159.call(this,heroId,weaponIdx,dungeonHeroTarget159(heroId,targetEnemyId));
};

const basic159=window.dungeonCombatBasicAttack;
window.dungeonCombatBasicAttack=function(heroId,targetEnemyId){
  return basic159.call(this,heroId,dungeonHeroTarget159(heroId,targetEnemyId));
};

const talent159=window.dungeonCombatUseActiveTalent;
window.dungeonCombatUseActiveTalent=function(heroId,talentId,targetId){
  try{
    const t=dungeonUnlockedActiveTalents(heroId).find(x=>String(x.id)===String(talentId));
    const fx=t?.effects||[];
    const needsEnemy=fx.some(dungeonTalentEffectNeedsEnemy);
    const needsAlly=fx.some(dungeonTalentEffectNeedsAlly);
    if(needsEnemy&&!needsAlly)targetId=dungeonHeroTarget159(heroId,targetId);
  }catch(e){}
  return talent159.call(this,heroId,talentId,targetId);
};

/* neutralise la cible globale de V158 pour éviter le doublon */
window.dungeonPlayerEnemyTarget158=null;
window.dungeonSelectEnemyTarget158=function(){};

/* rendu : pendant le tour d'un héros, cliquer sur UNE créature ennemie
   définit la cible de CE héros uniquement. */
const render159=window.renderDungeonCombatRound;
window.renderDungeonCombatRound=function(){
  const r=render159.apply(this,arguments);
  if(!dungeonCombatActive)return r;

  const cur=dungeonCurrentTurn156?.();
  const heroId=cur?.kind==="hero"?String(cur.id):null;
  const host=document.getElementById("dungeonCombatSetupView");
  if(!host)return r;

  /* retire l'ancien encadré "Cible de l'équipe" de V158 */
  host.querySelectorAll(".dungeonPlayerTargetBox158").forEach(x=>x.remove());

  const enemies=loadActiveEnemies()
    .filter(e=>(dungeonCombatSelection?.enemies||[]).includes(e.id)&&!e.defeated&&Number(e.hp)>0)
    .sort((a,b)=>(dungeonEnemyDerivedForInstance(b)?.initiative||0)-(dungeonEnemyDerivedForInstance(a)?.initiative||0));

  if(heroId){
    const currentTarget=dungeonHeroTarget159(heroId,null);

    document.querySelectorAll("#combatEnemySide .combatantCard").forEach((card,i)=>{
      const e=enemies[i];if(!e)return;
      const eid=String(e.id);

      card.classList.remove("dungeonPlayerTarget158");
      card.classList.toggle("dungeonHeroTarget159",String(currentTarget||"")===eid);
      card.style.cursor="crosshair";

      card.onclick=(ev)=>{
        if(ev.target?.closest?.(".dungeonCombatStatsBtn"))return;
        dungeonSelectHeroEnemyTarget159(heroId,eid);
      };
    });

    /* Les vieux selects ne sont plus une interaction joueur.
       On garde leur valeur synchronisée car les fonctions historiques l'utilisent encore. */
    document.querySelectorAll("#combatHeroSide .combatantCard").forEach(card=>{
      const hid=String(card.dataset.actorId||"");
      const sel=card.querySelector(".dungeonCombatTargetSelect");
      if(!sel)return;
      const t=dungeonHeroTarget159(hid,sel.value);
      if(t&&[...sel.options].some(o=>String(o.value)===String(t)))sel.value=t;
      sel.classList.add("dungeonTargetSelectLegacy159");
    });

    let box=host.querySelector(".dungeonHeroTargetBox159");
    if(!box){
      box=document.createElement("div");
      box.className="dungeonHeroTargetBox159";
      const tl=document.getElementById("dungeonTimeline156");
      if(tl)tl.insertAdjacentElement("afterend",box);
      else host.prepend(box);
    }

    const e=enemies.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(currentTarget||""));
    const d=e?activeEnemyDefinition(e.enemyId):null;
    const heroName=CHARS[heroId]?.name||heroId;

    box.innerHTML=e
      ? `🎯 <b>${z40kEscHtml(heroName)}</b> cible <b>${z40kEscHtml(d?.name||"Créature")}</b> · ❤️ ${Number(e.hp)||0}/${Number(e.maxHp)||0}`
      : `🎯 <b>${z40kEscHtml(heroName)}</b> : clique sur une créature ennemie pour choisir sa cible`;
  }else{
    host.querySelector(".dungeonHeroTargetBox159")?.remove();
    document.querySelectorAll("#combatEnemySide .combatantCard").forEach(card=>{
      card.classList.remove("dungeonHeroTarget159");
    });
  }

  /* nettoie les cibles mortes */
  Object.keys(dungeonHeroTargets159).forEach(hid=>{
    const eid=dungeonHeroTargets159[hid];
    const alive=enemies.some(e=>String(e.id)===String(eid));
    if(!alive)delete dungeonHeroTargets159[hid];
  });

  return r;
};

})();
</script>
<style id="rpgHeroTargeting159Css">
.dungeonHeroTarget159{
  outline:3px solid #57b8e8!important;
  box-shadow:0 0 16px #57b8e866!important
}
.dungeonHeroTargetBox159{
  margin:3px 0 10px;
  padding:8px 10px;
  border:1px solid #3f7890;
  border-radius:8px;
  background:#12232b;
  font-size:11px;
  font-weight:800
}
/* le select historique existe encore comme support moteur, mais ne pollue plus l'UI */
.dungeonTargetSelectLegacy159{display:none!important}
</style>


<script id="dungeonEconomy160">
(function(){
"use strict";

/* =========================================================
   V16.160 — Dungeon économie / loot / coffres
   ========================================================= */

const DUNGEON_ECO_RULES_160="gensrpg_dungeon_economy_rules_160";

window.dungeonEconomyRules160=function(){
  const d={
    chestMode:"discovered",       // discovered | free | disabled
    randomSearchChance:35,
    merchantMode:"event",         // event | always | disabled
    sellLootEnabled:true
  };
  try{Object.assign(d,JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(DUNGEON_ECO_RULES_160)||"{}"))}catch(e){}
  return d;
};
window.saveDungeonEconomyRules160=function(r){
  localStorage.setItem(DUNGEON_ECO_RULES_160,JSON.stringify(r||dungeonEconomyRules160()));
};

window.dungeonSessionEco160=function(){
  const id=String(activeGameProfileId()||"dungeon");
  const key="gensrpg_dungeon_session_eco_160_"+id;
  let d={chests:0,merchantPasses:0};
  try{Object.assign(d,JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||"{}"))}catch(e){}
  return {key,...d};
};
window.saveDungeonSessionEco160=function(d){
  if(!d?.key)return;
  const {key,...rest}=d;
  localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(rest));
};

/* ---------- objets de butin à vendre ---------- */
window.dungeonLootCatalog160=function(){
  return [
    {id:"dloot_old_coin",name:"Pièces anciennes",type:"Butin",price:18,rarity:"Commun",description:"Ancienne monnaie. Sans utilité directe, mais facile à revendre."},
    {id:"dloot_beast_fang",name:"Croc de bête",type:"Butin",price:28,rarity:"Commun",description:"Trophée récupéré sur une créature."},
    {id:"dloot_silver_idol",name:"Idole d'argent",type:"Butin",price:55,rarity:"Peu commun",description:"Petite statuette recherchée par les collectionneurs."},
    {id:"dloot_runic_shard",name:"Éclat runique",type:"Butin",price:75,rarity:"Peu commun",description:"Fragment chargé d'une ancienne magie."},
    {id:"dloot_black_pearl",name:"Perle noire",type:"Butin",price:110,rarity:"Rare",description:"Perle sombre extrêmement recherchée par les marchands."},
    {id:"dloot_dragon_scale",name:"Écaille draconique",type:"Butin",price:145,rarity:"Rare",description:"Matériau précieux provenant d'une créature exceptionnelle."},
    {id:"dloot_royal_relic",name:"Relique royale",type:"Butin",price:240,rarity:"Épique",description:"Objet ancien de grande valeur."},
    {id:"dloot_void_gem",name:"Gemme du Néant",type:"Butin",price:380,rarity:"Légendaire",description:"Gemme rarissime dont la valeur est considérable."}
  ];
};

window.dungeonEnsureLoot160=function(){
  if(typeof ITEMS==="undefined")return;
  const ids=new Set(ITEMS.map(x=>String(x.id)));
  dungeonLootCatalog160().forEach(it=>{
    if(!ids.has(it.id))ITEMS.push({...it,gameMode:"dungeon",contentFamily:"rpg",dungeonBuiltin:true,lootOnly:true});
  });
};
dungeonEnsureLoot160();

window.dungeonRollSellLoot160=function(){
  dungeonEnsureLoot160();
  const pool=dungeonLootCatalog160();
  const r=Math.random();
  let rarity=r<.52?"Commun":r<.80?"Peu commun":r<.95?"Rare":r<.992?"Épique":"Légendaire";
  const eligible=pool.filter(x=>x.rarity===rarity);
  return eligible[Math.floor(Math.random()*eligible.length)]||pool[0];
};

window.dungeonGiveItem160=function(heroId,it){
  if(!heroId||!it)return false;
  const st=loadState(heroId);
  st.inventory=Array.isArray(st.inventory)?st.inventory:[];
  st.inventory.push(makeInventoryEntry(it.id));
  localStorage.setItem(key(heroId),JSON.stringify(st));
  if(String(current||"")===String(heroId)){state=st;try{renderInventory();renderHands();renderGear()}catch(e){}}
  return true;
};

/* 70% trésor à vendre, 30% objet normal du pool Dungeon */
window.dungeonGrantChestLoot160=function(heroId=current||dungeonRandomHero()){
  if(!heroId)return null;
  let it=null;
  if(Math.random()<.70){
    it=dungeonRollSellLoot160();
  }else{
    const allowed=allowedObjectIdsForActiveProfile?.();
    const pool=(typeof ITEMS!=="undefined"?ITEMS:[]).filter(x=>
      !x.lootOnly && Number(x.price||0)>=0 && (!allowed||allowed.has(String(x.id)))
    );
    it=pool[Math.floor(Math.random()*pool.length)]||dungeonRollSellLoot160();
  }
  if(!it)return null;
  dungeonGiveItem160(heroId,it);
  return {heroId,item:it};
};

/* ---------- coffre : règle MJ ---------- */
window.openDungeonChest=function(){
  if(!isDungeonHeroSheet())return;
  const rules=dungeonEconomyRules160();
  if(rules.chestMode==="disabled"){
    alert("Les coffres / fouilles sont désactivés par le MJ.");
    return;
  }

  const sess=dungeonSessionEco160();
  if(rules.chestMode==="discovered"){
    if(Number(sess.chests)<=0){
      alert("Aucun coffre n'a été découvert pour le moment.");
      return;
    }
    sess.chests=Math.max(0,Number(sess.chests)-1);
    saveDungeonSessionEco160(sess);
  }else if(rules.chestMode==="free"){
    const chance=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(rules.randomSearchChance)||0));
    if(Math.random()*100>=chance){
      showEffectPopup("🔍","FOUILLE","<strong>Rien trouvé.</strong>","La zone ne contient rien d'intéressant.",false);
      updateDungeonSearchUi();
      return;
    }
  }

  const drop=dungeonGrantChestLoot160(current);
  if(drop){
    const sell=Number(drop.item?.price)||0;
    showEffectPopup("🎁","COFFRE / FOUILLE",
      "<strong>"+z40kEscHtml(drop.item?.name||"Objet")+"</strong>"+
      (drop.item?.lootOnly?"<br><small>Butin destiné principalement à la vente · valeur "+gensCurrencyLabel(sell)+"</small>":""),
      "Objet ajouté à l'inventaire.",false);
  }
  updateDungeonSearchUi();
};

const oldUpdateSearch160=window.updateDungeonSearchUi;
window.updateDungeonSearchUi=function(){
  oldUpdateSearch160?.();
  if(!isDungeonHeroSheet())return;
  const c=document.getElementById("dungeonChestBtn");
  if(!c)return;
  const rules=dungeonEconomyRules160(),sess=dungeonSessionEco160();
  if(rules.chestMode==="disabled"){
    c.style.display="none";
  }else if(rules.chestMode==="discovered"){
    c.style.display="block";
    c.disabled=Number(sess.chests)<=0;
    c.textContent=Number(sess.chests)>0?"🎁 OUVRIR UN COFFRE · "+sess.chests:"🎁 AUCUN COFFRE DÉCOUVERT";
  }else{
    c.style.display="block";
    c.disabled=false;
    c.textContent="🔍 FOUILLER LA ZONE";
  }
};

/* MJ peut rendre un coffre disponible ponctuellement */
window.dungeonMjAddChest160=function(n=1){
  if(!isGameMasterDevice?.())return;
  const s=dungeonSessionEco160();
  s.chests=Math.max(0,Number(s.chests)||0)+Math.max(1,Number(n)||1);
  saveDungeonSessionEco160(s);
  try{updateDungeonSearchUi()}catch(e){}
  alert("Coffre disponible pour le groupe. Total : "+s.chests);
};

/* ---------- marchand ---------- */
window.dungeonMerchantAvailable160=function(){
  const r=dungeonEconomyRules160(),s=dungeonSessionEco160();
  return r.merchantMode==="always" || (r.merchantMode==="event"&&Number(s.merchantPasses)>0);
};
window.dungeonOpenMerchant160=function(){
  const r=dungeonEconomyRules160(),s=dungeonSessionEco160();
  if(r.merchantMode==="disabled"){alert("Les marchands sont désactivés par le MJ.");return}
  if(r.merchantMode==="event"&&Number(s.merchantPasses)<=0){alert("Aucun marchand n'est actuellement présent.");return}
  if(r.merchantMode==="event"){
    s.merchantPasses=Math.max(0,Number(s.merchantPasses)-1);
    saveDungeonSessionEco160(s);
  }
  openMerchant();
};
window.dungeonMjActivateMerchant160=function(){
  if(!isGameMasterDevice?.())return;
  const s=dungeonSessionEco160();s.merchantPasses=Math.max(0,Number(s.merchantPasses)||0)+1;saveDungeonSessionEco160(s);
  alert("Un marchand est disponible pour la prochaine ouverture.");
  dungeonRenderEconomyButtons160();
};

/* Un événement marchand autorise aussi l'ouverture depuis le hub. */
const oldOpenMerchant160=window.openMerchant;
window.openMerchant=function(){
  if(isDungeonMode()){
    const r=dungeonEconomyRules160(),s=dungeonSessionEco160();
    if(r.merchantMode==="event" && Number(s.merchantPasses)<=0){
      /* appel provenant d'un événement : donne un accès */
      s.merchantPasses=1;saveDungeonSessionEco160(s);
    }
  }
  return oldOpenMerchant160.apply(this,arguments);
};

/* bouton marchand propre au hub Dungeon */
window.dungeonRenderEconomyButtons160=function(){
  if(!isDungeonMode())return;
  const panel=document.getElementById("dungeonMenuPanel");
  if(!panel)return;

  let b=document.getElementById("dungeonMerchantBtn160");
  const rules=dungeonEconomyRules160();
  if(rules.merchantMode==="disabled"){b?.remove();return}

  if(!b){
    b=document.createElement("button");
    b.id="dungeonMerchantBtn160";
    b.className="dungeonMjBtn";
    b.onclick=dungeonOpenMerchant160;
    const mj=document.getElementById("dungeonMjMenuBtn");
    if(mj)mj.insertAdjacentElement("afterend",b);else panel.appendChild(b);
  }
  const s=dungeonSessionEco160();
  const available=dungeonMerchantAvailable160();
  b.disabled=!available;
  b.textContent=rules.merchantMode==="always"?"🛒 MARCHAND":available?"🛒 MARCHAND DISPONIBLE":"🛒 AUCUN MARCHAND";
};

/* ---------- Réglages MJ Dungeon ---------- */
const oldOpenMj160=window.openDungeonMj151;
window.openDungeonMj151=function(){
  oldOpenMj160.apply(this,arguments);
  const grid=document.querySelector("#dungeonMj151 .gensMjGrid151");
  if(!grid||document.getElementById("dmjEco160"))return;

  const sec=document.createElement("section");
  sec.id="dmjEco160";
  sec.innerHTML=`<h3>🎁 Fouille, coffres & marchand</h3>
    <label>Accès aux coffres / fouilles
      <select id="dmjChestMode160">
        <option value="discovered">Seulement quand un coffre est découvert</option>
        <option value="free">Fouille aléatoire autorisée</option>
        <option value="disabled">Désactivé</option>
      </select>
    </label>
    <label id="dmjSearchChanceWrap160">Chance de trouver quelque chose (%)
      <input id="dmjSearchChance160" type="number" min="0" max="100" step="5">
    </label>
    <label>Marchand
      <select id="dmjMerchantMode160">
        <option value="event">Seulement événement / activation MJ</option>
        <option value="always">Toujours accessible</option>
        <option value="disabled">Désactivé</option>
      </select>
    </label>
    <div class="gensMjQuick160">
      <button type="button" onclick="dungeonMjAddChest160(1)">🎁 RENDRE 1 COFFRE DISPONIBLE</button>
      <button type="button" onclick="dungeonMjActivateMerchant160()">🛒 FAIRE APPARAÎTRE UN MARCHAND</button>
    </div>`;

  grid.appendChild(sec);
  const r=dungeonEconomyRules160();
  document.getElementById("dmjChestMode160").value=r.chestMode;
  document.getElementById("dmjSearchChance160").value=r.randomSearchChance;
  document.getElementById("dmjMerchantMode160").value=r.merchantMode;
};

const oldSaveMj160=window.saveDungeonMj151;
window.saveDungeonMj151=function(){
  const chest=document.getElementById("dmjChestMode160");
  if(chest){
    const r=dungeonEconomyRules160();
    r.chestMode=chest.value||"discovered";
    r.randomSearchChance=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(document.getElementById("dmjSearchChance160")?.value)||0));
    r.merchantMode=document.getElementById("dmjMerchantMode160")?.value||"event";
    saveDungeonEconomyRules160(r);
  }
  oldSaveMj160.apply(this,arguments);
  try{updateDungeonSearchUi();dungeonRenderEconomyButtons160()}catch(e){}
};

/* récompense combat Dungeon : petite chance de butin de vente */
const oldVictory160=window.openDungeonCombatVictoryPopup;
if(typeof oldVictory160==="function"){
  window.openDungeonCombatVictoryPopup=function(){
    const r=oldVictory160.apply(this,arguments);
    try{
      if(dungeonCombatActive && !dungeonCombatRewards?._sellLoot160 && Math.random()<.45){
        const hero=dungeonRandomHero();
        const it=dungeonRollSellLoot160();
        if(hero&&it){
          dungeonGiveItem160(hero,it);
          dungeonCombatRewards._sellLoot160=true;
          setTimeout(()=>showEffectPopup("💰","BUTIN",
            "<strong>"+z40kEscHtml(it.name)+"</strong><br><small>Valeur de revente : "+gensCurrencyLabel(it.price)+"</small>",
            (CHARS[hero]?.name||hero)+" récupère un objet de valeur.",false),350);
        }
      }
    }catch(e){}
    return r;
  };
}

/* refresh hub */
const oldMenu160=window.renderMenuStatuses;
window.renderMenuStatuses=function(){
  const r=oldMenu160.apply(this,arguments);
  try{dungeonRenderEconomyButtons160()}catch(e){}
  return r;
};

setTimeout(()=>{try{dungeonEnsureLoot160();dungeonRenderEconomyButtons160()}catch(e){}},100);
})();
</script>

<script id="dungeonLootCleanup161">
(function(){
"use strict";

/* =========================================================
   V16.161 — une seule source de loot en combat Dungeon
   - Combat = table de loot du monstre uniquement.
   - Coffres / fouilles / événements = tables séparées.
   - Suppression du bonus aléatoire de fin de combat V160.
   - Tables de loot cohérentes pour les 20 monstres de la démo.
   ========================================================= */

window.dungeonDemoLootTables161={
 "dng_skeleton":[
   {itemId:"dloot_old_coin",chance:28,qty:1},
   {itemId:"dng_heal_potion",chance:4,qty:1}
 ],
 "dng_skeleton_archer":[
   {itemId:"dloot_old_coin",chance:24,qty:1},
   {itemId:"dng_bow",chance:2,qty:1}
 ],
 "dng_skeleton_guard":[
   {itemId:"dloot_silver_idol",chance:15,qty:1},
   {itemId:"dng_shield",chance:3,qty:1}
 ],
 "dng_ghoul":[
   {itemId:"dloot_beast_fang",chance:32,qty:1},
   {itemId:"dng_heal_potion",chance:5,qty:1}

window.dungeonApplyDemoLootTables161=function(){
  try{dungeonEnsureLoot160?.()}catch(e){}
  try{ensureDungeonContent?.()}catch(e){}

  Object.entries(dungeonDemoLootTables161).forEach(([id,loot])=>{
    const z=ZOMBIE_TYPES.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id));
    if(!z)return;
    z.rule=z.rule||{};
    z.rule.loot=loot.map(x=>({...x}));
    z.rule.goldDrop={...(dungeonDemoGoldDrops161[id]||{chance:0,min:0,max:0})};
  });

  /* Le profil démo utilise les pourcentages écrits dans les tables tels quels.
     Les univers custom gardent leurs propres réglages. */
  try{
    const profiles=loadGameProfilesRaw();
    const i=profiles.findIndex(p=>String(p?.id)===String(GAME_PROFILE_DUNGEON_ID));
    if(i>=0){
      profiles[i].enemyLoot={...(profiles[i].enemyLoot||{}),enabled:true,multiplier:1};
      saveGameProfiles(profiles);
    }
  }catch(e){}
};
dungeonApplyDemoLootTables161();

/* Réapplique après reconstruction runtime des ennemis intégrés. */
const ensure161=window.ensureDungeonContent;
if(typeof ensure161==="function"){
  window.ensureDungeonContent=function(){
    const r=ensure161.apply(this,arguments);
    Object.entries(dungeonDemoLootTables161).forEach(([id,loot])=>{
      const z=ZOMBIE_TYPES.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id));
      if(z){
        z.rule=z.rule||{};
        z.rule.loot=loot.map(x=>({...x}));
        z.rule.goldDrop={...(dungeonDemoGoldDrops161[id]||{chance:0,min:0,max:0})};
      }
    });
    return r;
  };
}

/* COMBAT : plus aucun "loot bonus" aléatoire de victoire.
   La récompense affichée est exclusivement celle obtenue depuis les tables des monstres. */
window.openDungeonCombatVictoryPopup=function(){
  if(!dungeonCombatActive||dungeonCombatRewards.shown)return;
  dungeonCombatRewards.shown=true;
  const m=document.getElementById("dungeonCombatVictoryModal");
  const b=document.getElementById("dungeonCombatVictoryBody");
  if(!m||!b)return;
  b.innerHTML=dungeonCombatVictoryHtml();
  m.style.display="block";
};

/* Texte de victoire plus explicite sur la provenance. */
const oldVictoryHtml161=window.dungeonCombatVictoryHtml;
window.dungeonCombatVictoryHtml=function(){
  const html=oldVictoryHtml161.apply(this,arguments);
  return html
    .replace("<h3>🎁 Drops obtenus</h3>","<h3>🎁 Butin des monstres</h3>")
    .replace("Aucun drop obtenu.","Aucun objet obtenu sur les tables de loot des monstres.");
};

/* Affichage lisible dans le bestiaire/éditeur : source exacte des drops.
   Ce helper peut aussi être réutilisé plus tard dans une fiche info. */
window.dungeonLootTableText161=function(enemyId){
  const z=ZOMBIE_TYPES.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(enemyId));
  const loot=z?.rule?.loot||[];
  const gold=z?.rule?.goldDrop;
  const rows=loot.map(l=>{
    const it=itemById(l.itemId);
    return `${it?.name||l.itemId} — ${Number(l.chance)||0}%${Number(l.qty)>1?" ×"+l.qty:""}`;
  });
  if(gold&&Number(gold.chance)>0)
    rows.push(`Or direct — ${Number(gold.chance)}% · ${Number(gold.min)||0} à ${Number(gold.max)||0}`);
  return rows.length?rows.join(" · "):"Aucun loot configuré";
};

})();
</script>

function mc162MergeById(existing,seed){
  const out=Array.isArray(existing)?[...existing]:[];
  const ids=new Set(out.map(x=>String(x?.id||"")));
  (seed||[]).forEach(x=>{
    const id=String(x?.id||"");
    if(id&&!ids.has(id)){out.push(x);ids.add(id)}
  });
  return out;
}

window.ensureBuiltinMonsterCapture162=function(){
  /* Profil */
  try{
    const profiles=loadGameProfilesRaw();
    if(!profiles.some(p=>String(p?.id)===MC162_ID)){
      profiles.push(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(MC162_PROFILE)));
      saveGameProfiles(profiles);
    }
  }catch(e){console.warn("Seed profil Monster Capture",e)}

  /* Dresseur de démonstration */
  try{
    const heroes=loadCustomHeroesMulti();
    if(!heroes.some(h=>String(h?.id)===String(MC162_TRAINER.id))){
      heroes.push(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(MC162_TRAINER)));
      saveCustomHeroesMulti(heroes);
    }
  }catch(e){console.warn("Seed dresseur Capture",e)}

  /* Roster de créatures : fusion par ID, jamais d'écrasement utilisateur. */
  try{
    const familyKey="gensrpg_shared_entities_v1__family__creature";
    let current=[];
    try{current=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(familyKey)||"[]")}catch(e){}
    const merged=mc162MergeById(current,MC162_ENTITIES);
    if(merged.length!==current.length)localStorage.setItem(familyKey,JSON.stringify(merged));

    const scopedKey="gensrpg_shared_entities_v1__"+MC162_ID;
    let scoped=[];
    try{scoped=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(scopedKey)||"[]")}catch(e){}
    const mergedScoped=mc162MergeById(scoped,MC162_ENTITIES);
    if(mergedScoped.length!==scoped.length)localStorage.setItem(scopedKey,JSON.stringify(mergedScoped));

    localStorage.setItem("gensrpg_shared_entities_scoped_v1__family__creature","1");
    localStorage.setItem("gensrpg_shared_entities_scoped_v1__"+MC162_ID,"1");
  }catch(e){console.warn("Seed roster Capture",e)}

  /* Bibliothèque de capacités : on ajoute uniquement les IDs manquants. */
  try{
    const key="gensrpg_ability_library_v1";
    let current=[];
    try{current=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||"[]")}catch(e){}
    const merged=mc162MergeById(current,MC162_ABILITIES);
    if(merged.length!==current.length)localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(merged));
  }catch(e){console.warn("Seed capacités Capture",e)}

  /* Configuration des modules du profil. */
  try{
    const key="gensrpg_rpg_gameplay_by_profile_v1";
    let map={};
    try{map=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||"{}")||{}}catch(e){}
    if(!map[MC162_ID]){
      map[MC162_ID]=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(MC162_GAMEPLAY));
      localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(map));
    }
  }catch(e){console.warn("Seed gameplay Capture",e)}

  /* Réglages Capture de démonstration — seulement s'ils n'existent pas. */
  try{
    Object.entries(MC162_RULE_KEYS).forEach(([k,v])=>{
      if(localStorage.getItem(k)==null)localStorage.setItem(k,v);
    });
  }catch(e){console.warn("Seed règles Capture",e)}

  try{refreshCustomEquipmentIntoItems?.()}catch(e){}
  try{applyCustomHeroesMulti?.()}catch(e){}
  try{renderGameProfileLibrary?.()}catch(e){}
};

ensureBuiltinMonsterCapture162();
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",()=>setTimeout(ensureBuiltinMonsterCapture162,60));
}

--- minPhysicalDamage in resolve036 ---
function resolve036(inst,def,skill,targetHeroId){
  const profile=dungeonEnemyAttackProfile(def,skill);
  const rules=loadDungeonRpgRules();
  const target=dungeonDerivedForHero(targetHeroId);
  const dice=Math.max(1,Number(profile?.dice)||1);
  let total=0,hits=0,dodges=0,crits=0;
  const rolls=[];

  for(let i=0;i<dice;i++){
    const defPenalty=Math.max(0,
      ((Number(target.defense)||0)-(Number(profile.attributeValue)||0))*
      Number(rules.defensePenaltyPerPoint||0)
    );
    const chance=dungeonClampHitChance((Number(profile.preDefenseChance)||0)-defPenalty);
    const threshold=d100ThresholdFromChance(chance);
    const roll=Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1;
    const rd={roll,threshold,chance,hit:false,crit:false,dodged:false,damage:0};

    if(roll<threshold){rolls.push(rd);continue}
    hits++;rd.hit=true;

    const critThreshold=Math.max(1,101-(Number(profile.crit)||0));
    const crit=(Number(profile.crit)||0)>0 && roll>=critThreshold;
    if(crit){crits++;rd.crit=true}

    const raw=Math.round((Number(profile.power)||0)*
      (crit?Number(rules.critMultiplier||2):1));
    const mult=gensElementMultiplier(profile.element,gensHeroElementTypes(targetHeroId));
    const elemental=Math.max(0,Math.round(raw*mult));

    const dodge=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(target.dodge)||0));
    if(dodge>0 && (Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1)>=Math.max(1,101-dodge)){
      dodges++;rd.dodged=true;rolls.push(rd);continue;
    }

    const mit=dungeonMitigationForHero(targetHeroId,profile.damageType);
    const reduction=Math.max(0,Number(mit.reduction)||0);
    const configuredMin=Math.max(0,Number(mit.min)||0);
    /* Une touche ayant encore une valeur >0 après l'élément ne peut pas être
       annulée totalement par l'armure. Les anciennes sauvegardes pouvaient
       conserver minPhysicalDamage/minMagicDamage à 0. */
    let final=elemental>0
      ? Math.max(1,configuredMin,elemental-reduction)
      : 0;
    rd.rawDamage=raw;
    rd.elementalDamage=elemental;
    rd.reduction=reduction;
    rd.minDamage=Math.max(1,configuredMin);
    rd.damage=Math.max(0,final);
    total+=rd.damage;
    rolls.push(rd);
  }

  let applied=0;
  if(total>0 && targetHeroId){
    try{
      const s=loadState(targetHeroId);
      const maxHp=dungeonMaxHpForHero(targetHeroId,s);
      const beforeWounds=Math.max(0,Number(s.wounds)||0);
      const beforeHp=Math.max(0,maxHp-beforeWounds);
      s.wounds=Math.min(maxHp,beforeWounds+Math.max(0,Math.round(total)));
      localStorage.setItem(key(targetHeroId),JSON.stringify(s));
      const afterHp=Math.max(0,maxHp-(Number(s.wounds)||0));
      applied=Math.max(0,beforeHp-afterHp);
      try{if(typeof z40kSchedulePush==="function")z40kSchedulePush()}catch(e){}
    }catch(e){console.warn("AI damage 0.42",e)}
  }

  return {
    dice,hits,dodges,crits,total:applied,calculatedTotal:total,
    skill:skill?.name||"Attaque",
    rolls,
    profile:{
      chance:Number(profile?.chance ?? profile?.preDefenseChance ?? 0),
      power:Number(profile?.power||0),
      crit:Number(profile?.crit||0),
      range:profile?.range ?? "—",
      damageType:profile?.damageType||"physique",
      element:profile?.element||"none"
    }
  };
}

===== FUNCTIONS CONTAINING armorReductionGain =====

--- armorReductionGain in defaultDungeonRpgRules ---
function defaultDungeonRpgRules(){
  return {
    xpPerLevel:10,skillPointsPerLevel:1,startingSkillPoints:0,
    physicalDamageStep:10,physicalDamageGain:1,
    physicalDamageFormula:"step",physicalDamagePercentPerPoint:0,
    magicDamageStep:10,magicDamageGain:1,
    magicDamageFormula:"step",magicDamagePercentPerPoint:0,
    rangedDamageFormula:"none",rangedDamagePercentPerPoint:0,
    hpFormula:"step",hpPercentPerPoint:0,
    manaFormula:"step",manaPercentPerPoint:0,
    critFormula:"step",critPercentPerPoint:0,
    dodgeFormula:"step",dodgePercentPerPoint:0,
    initiativeFormula:"step",initiativePerPoint:0,
    rulesComplexity:"simple",initiativeUsage:"tests",initiativeTurnMode:"fixed",initiativeDie:20,testsEnabled:true,testDie:20,testDefaultDifficulty:10,
    magicResistFormula:"step",magicResistPerPoint:0,
    meleeHitStep:10,meleeHitGain:5,
    rangedHitStep:10,rangedHitGain:5,
    magicHitStep:10,magicHitGain:5,
    basicAttackBaseChance:50,
    baseMana:0,spiritManaStep:10,manaGain:10,magicAttackManaCost:1,
    baseCrit:0,agilityCritStep:10,critGain:5,critCap:50,critMultiplier:2,
    baseDodge:0,agilityDodgeStep:10,dodgeGain:3,dodgeCap:40,
    baseInitiative:0,agilityInitiativeStep:9999,initiativeGain:0,
    enduranceHpStep:10,hpGain:2,
    defensePenaltyPerPoint:1,hitChanceMin:5,hitChanceMax:95,
    armorReductionStep:2,armorReductionGain:1,minPhysicalDamage:1,
    spiritMagicResistStep:20,magicResistGain:1,minMagicDamage:1
  };
}

--- armorReductionGain in normalizeDungeonRpgRules ---
function normalizeDungeonRpgRules(r){
  const d=defaultDungeonRpgRules(),o={...d,...(r||{})};
  const pos=(k,min=1,max=9999)=>o[k]=Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Number(o[k])||d[k]));
  const non=(k,max=9999)=>o[k]=Math.max(0,Math.min(max,Number(o[k])||0));
  pos("xpPerLevel");non("skillPointsPerLevel",99);non("startingSkillPoints",99);
  pos("physicalDamageStep");non("physicalDamageGain",99);
  o.physicalDamageFormula=o.physicalDamageFormula==="percent"?"percent":"step";non("physicalDamagePercentPerPoint",100);
  pos("magicDamageStep");non("magicDamageGain",99);
  o.magicDamageFormula=o.magicDamageFormula==="percent"?"percent":"step";non("magicDamagePercentPerPoint",100);
  o.rangedDamageFormula=o.rangedDamageFormula==="percent"?"percent":"none";non("rangedDamagePercentPerPoint",100);
  o.hpFormula=o.hpFormula==="percent"?"percent":"step";non("hpPercentPerPoint",100);
  o.manaFormula=o.manaFormula==="percent"?"percent":"step";non("manaPercentPerPoint",100);
  o.critFormula=o.critFormula==="perPoint"?"perPoint":"step";non("critPercentPerPoint",100);
  o.dodgeFormula=o.dodgeFormula==="perPoint"?"perPoint":"step";non("dodgePercentPerPoint",100);
  o.initiativeFormula=o.initiativeFormula==="perPoint"?"perPoint":"step";non("initiativePerPoint",100);
  o.magicResistFormula=o.magicResistFormula==="perPoint"?"perPoint":"step";non("magicResistPerPoint",100);
  o.rulesComplexity=["simple","tactical","rpg","custom"].includes(o.rulesComplexity)?o.rulesComplexity:"simple";
  o.initiativeUsage=["off","turns","tests","both","custom"].includes(o.initiativeUsage)?o.initiativeUsage:"tests";
  o.initiativeTurnMode=["fixed","die","low"].includes(o.initiativeTurnMode)?o.initiativeTurnMode:"fixed";
  o.initiativeDie=[4,6,8,10,12,20,100].includes(Number(o.initiativeDie))?Number(o.initiativeDie):20;
  o.testsEnabled=o.testsEnabled!==false; o.testDie=[4,6,8,10,12,20,100].includes(Number(o.testDie))?Number(o.testDie):20;
  o.testDefaultDifficulty=Math.max(1,Math.min(999,Number(o.testDefaultDifficulty)||10));
  pos("meleeHitStep");non("meleeHitGain",99);
  pos("rangedHitStep");non("rangedHitGain",99);
  pos("magicHitStep");non("magicHitGain",99);
  o.basicAttackBaseChance=Math.max(1,Math.min(99,Number(o.basicAttackBaseChance)||d.basicAttackBaseChance));
  non("baseMana");pos("spiritManaStep");non("manaGain");non("magicAttackManaCost");
  non("baseCrit",100);pos("agilityCritStep");non("critGain",100);non("critCap",100);
  o.critMultiplier=Math.max(1,Math.min(10,Number(o.critMultiplier)||d.critMultiplier));
  non("baseDodge",100);pos("agilityDodgeStep");non("dodgeGain",100);non("dodgeCap",100);
  o.baseInitiative=Math.max(-99,Math.min(999,Number(o.baseInitiative)||0));
  pos("agilityInitiativeStep");non("initiativeGain",99);
  pos("enduranceHpStep");non("hpGain",999);
  non("defensePenaltyPerPoint",25);
  o.hitChanceMin=Math.max(0,Math.min(99,Number.isFinite(Number(o.hitChanceMin))?Number(o.hitChanceMin):d.hitChanceMin));
  o.hitChanceMax=Math.max(o.hitChanceMin,Math.min(100,Number(o.hitChanceMax)||d.hitChanceMax));
  pos("armorReductionStep");non("armorReductionGain",99);non("minPhysicalDamage",99);
  pos("spiritMagicResistStep");non("magicResistGain",99);non("minMagicDamage",99);
  return o;
}

    drHpPctPerPoint:"hpPercentPerPoint",drManaPctPerPoint:"manaPercentPerPoint",
    drCritPerPoint:"critPercentPerPoint",drDodgePerPoint:"dodgePercentPerPoint",
    drInitiativePerPoint:"initiativePerPoint",drMagicResistPerPoint:"magicResistPerPoint",
    drMeleeHitStep:"meleeHitStep",drMeleeHitGain:"meleeHitGain",
    drRangedHitStep:"rangedHitStep",drRangedHitGain:"rangedHitGain",
    drMagicHitStep:"magicHitStep",drMagicHitGain:"magicHitGain",
    drBasicHitChance:"basicAttackBaseChance",drHitChanceMin:"hitChanceMin",drHitChanceMax:"hitChanceMax",drDefensePenalty:"defensePenaltyPerPoint",
    drBaseMana:"baseMana",drSpiritStep:"spiritManaStep",drManaGain:"manaGain",drMagicAttackCost:"magicAttackManaCost",
    drBaseCrit:"baseCrit",drCritStep:"agilityCritStep",drCritGain:"critGain",drCritCap:"critCap",drCritMultiplier:"critMultiplier",

    drBaseInitiative:"baseInitiative",drInitiativeStep:"agilityInitiativeStep",drInitiativeGain:"initiativeGain",
    drHpStep:"enduranceHpStep",drHpGain:"hpGain",
    drArmorStep:"armorReductionStep",drArmorGain:"armorReductionGain",drMinPhysicalDamage:"minPhysicalDamage",
    drMagicResistStep:"spiritMagicResistStep",drMagicResistGain:"magicResistGain",drMinMagicDamage:"minMagicDamage"
  };
  Object.entries(map).forEach(([id,k])=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=r[k]});
  const selects={
    drPhysicalFormula:r.physicalDamageFormula,drMagicFormula:r.magicDamageFormula,

    drMagicResistFormula:r.magicResistFormula
  };
  Object.entries(selects).forEach(([id,val])=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=val});
  [["drRulesComplexity",r.rulesComplexity],["drInitiativeUsage",r.initiativeUsage],["drInitiativeTurnMode",r.initiativeTurnMode],["drInitiativeDie",r.initiativeDie],["drTestDie",r.testDie]].forEach(([id,val])=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=String(val)});
  const te=document.getElementById("drTestsEnabled");if(te)te.checked=r.testsEnabled!==false; const td=document.getElementById("drTestDefaultDifficulty");if(td)td.value=r.testDefaultDifficulty; renderDungeonProgressiveRules();
  const p=document.getElementById("dungeonRulesEditorPreview");if(p)p.innerHTML="<strong>📖 Résumé joueur</strong>"+dungeonRpgRulesSummaryHtml();
}

--- armorReductionGain in dungeonArmorReductionFromScore ---
function dungeonArmorReductionFromScore(score){
  const r=loadDungeonRpgRules();
  return Math.max(0,Math.floor(Math.max(0,Number(score)||0)/Math.max(1,r.armorReductionStep))*Math.max(0,Number(r.armorReductionGain)||0));
}

===== FUNCTIONS CONTAINING armorReductionStep =====

--- armorReductionStep in defaultDungeonRpgRules ---
function defaultDungeonRpgRules(){
  return {
    xpPerLevel:10,skillPointsPerLevel:1,startingSkillPoints:0,
    physicalDamageStep:10,physicalDamageGain:1,
    physicalDamageFormula:"step",physicalDamagePercentPerPoint:0,
    magicDamageStep:10,magicDamageGain:1,
    magicDamageFormula:"step",magicDamagePercentPerPoint:0,
    rangedDamageFormula:"none",rangedDamagePercentPerPoint:0,
    hpFormula:"step",hpPercentPerPoint:0,
    manaFormula:"step",manaPercentPerPoint:0,
    critFormula:"step",critPercentPerPoint:0,
    dodgeFormula:"step",dodgePercentPerPoint:0,
    initiativeFormula:"step",initiativePerPoint:0,
    rulesComplexity:"simple",initiativeUsage:"tests",initiativeTurnMode:"fixed",initiativeDie:20,testsEnabled:true,testDie:20,testDefaultDifficulty:10,
    magicResistFormula:"step",magicResistPerPoint:0,
    meleeHitStep:10,meleeHitGain:5,
    rangedHitStep:10,rangedHitGain:5,
    magicHitStep:10,magicHitGain:5,
    basicAttackBaseChance:50,
    baseMana:0,spiritManaStep:10,manaGain:10,magicAttackManaCost:1,
    baseCrit:0,agilityCritStep:10,critGain:5,critCap:50,critMultiplier:2,
    baseDodge:0,agilityDodgeStep:10,dodgeGain:3,dodgeCap:40,
    baseInitiative:0,agilityInitiativeStep:9999,initiativeGain:0,
    enduranceHpStep:10,hpGain:2,
    defensePenaltyPerPoint:1,hitChanceMin:5,hitChanceMax:95,
    armorReductionStep:2,armorReductionGain:1,minPhysicalDamage:1,
    spiritMagicResistStep:20,magicResistGain:1,minMagicDamage:1
  };
}

--- armorReductionStep in normalizeDungeonRpgRules ---
function normalizeDungeonRpgRules(r){
  const d=defaultDungeonRpgRules(),o={...d,...(r||{})};
  const pos=(k,min=1,max=9999)=>o[k]=Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Number(o[k])||d[k]));
  const non=(k,max=9999)=>o[k]=Math.max(0,Math.min(max,Number(o[k])||0));
  pos("xpPerLevel");non("skillPointsPerLevel",99);non("startingSkillPoints",99);
  pos("physicalDamageStep");non("physicalDamageGain",99);
  o.physicalDamageFormula=o.physicalDamageFormula==="percent"?"percent":"step";non("physicalDamagePercentPerPoint",100);
  pos("magicDamageStep");non("magicDamageGain",99);
  o.magicDamageFormula=o.magicDamageFormula==="percent"?"percent":"step";non("magicDamagePercentPerPoint",100);
  o.rangedDamageFormula=o.rangedDamageFormula==="percent"?"percent":"none";non("rangedDamagePercentPerPoint",100);
  o.hpFormula=o.hpFormula==="percent"?"percent":"step";non("hpPercentPerPoint",100);
  o.manaFormula=o.manaFormula==="percent"?"percent":"step";non("manaPercentPerPoint",100);
  o.critFormula=o.critFormula==="perPoint"?"perPoint":"step";non("critPercentPerPoint",100);
  o.dodgeFormula=o.dodgeFormula==="perPoint"?"perPoint":"step";non("dodgePercentPerPoint",100);
  o.initiativeFormula=o.initiativeFormula==="perPoint"?"perPoint":"step";non("initiativePerPoint",100);
  o.magicResistFormula=o.magicResistFormula==="perPoint"?"perPoint":"step";non("magicResistPerPoint",100);
  o.rulesComplexity=["simple","tactical","rpg","custom"].includes(o.rulesComplexity)?o.rulesComplexity:"simple";
  o.initiativeUsage=["off","turns","tests","both","custom"].includes(o.initiativeUsage)?o.initiativeUsage:"tests";
  o.initiativeTurnMode=["fixed","die","low"].includes(o.initiativeTurnMode)?o.initiativeTurnMode:"fixed";
  o.initiativeDie=[4,6,8,10,12,20,100].includes(Number(o.initiativeDie))?Number(o.initiativeDie):20;
  o.testsEnabled=o.testsEnabled!==false; o.testDie=[4,6,8,10,12,20,100].includes(Number(o.testDie))?Number(o.testDie):20;
  o.testDefaultDifficulty=Math.max(1,Math.min(999,Number(o.testDefaultDifficulty)||10));
  pos("meleeHitStep");non("meleeHitGain",99);
  pos("rangedHitStep");non("rangedHitGain",99);
  pos("magicHitStep");non("magicHitGain",99);
  o.basicAttackBaseChance=Math.max(1,Math.min(99,Number(o.basicAttackBaseChance)||d.basicAttackBaseChance));
  non("baseMana");pos("spiritManaStep");non("manaGain");non("magicAttackManaCost");
  non("baseCrit",100);pos("agilityCritStep");non("critGain",100);non("critCap",100);
  o.critMultiplier=Math.max(1,Math.min(10,Number(o.critMultiplier)||d.critMultiplier));
  non("baseDodge",100);pos("agilityDodgeStep");non("dodgeGain",100);non("dodgeCap",100);
  o.baseInitiative=Math.max(-99,Math.min(999,Number(o.baseInitiative)||0));
  pos("agilityInitiativeStep");non("initiativeGain",99);
  pos("enduranceHpStep");non("hpGain",999);
  non("defensePenaltyPerPoint",25);
  o.hitChanceMin=Math.max(0,Math.min(99,Number.isFinite(Number(o.hitChanceMin))?Number(o.hitChanceMin):d.hitChanceMin));
  o.hitChanceMax=Math.max(o.hitChanceMin,Math.min(100,Number(o.hitChanceMax)||d.hitChanceMax));
  pos("armorReductionStep");non("armorReductionGain",99);non("minPhysicalDamage",99);
  pos("spiritMagicResistStep");non("magicResistGain",99);non("minMagicDamage",99);
  return o;
}

    drRangedDamageFormula:r.rangedDamageFormula,drHpFormula:r.hpFormula,drManaFormula:r.manaFormula,
    drCritFormula:r.critFormula,drDodgeFormula:r.dodgeFormula,drInitiativeFormula:r.initiativeFormula,
    drMagicResistFormula:r.magicResistFormula
  };
  Object.entries(selects).forEach(([id,val])=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=val});
  [["drRulesComplexity",r.rulesComplexity],["drInitiativeUsage",r.initiativeUsage],["drInitiativeTurnMode",r.initiativeTurnMode],["drInitiativeDie",r.initiativeDie],["drTestDie",r.testDie]].forEach(([id,val])=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=String(val)});
  const te=document.getElementById("drTestsEnabled");if(te)te.checked=r.testsEnabled!==false; const td=document.getElementById("drTestDefaultDifficulty");if(td)td.value=r.testDefaultDifficulty; renderDungeonProgressiveRules();
  const p=document.getElementById("dungeonRulesEditorPreview");if(p)p.innerHTML="<strong>📖 Résumé joueur</strong>"+dungeonRpgRulesSummaryHtml();
}

function readDungeonRpgRulesEditor(){
  const v=id=>Number(document.getElementById(id)?.value);
  return {
    rulesComplexity:document.getElementById("drRulesComplexity")?.value||"simple",initiativeUsage:document.getElementById("drInitiativeUsage")?.value||"tests",initiativeTurnMode:document.getElementById("drInitiativeTurnMode")?.value||"fixed",initiativeDie:v("drInitiativeDie"),testsEnabled:!!document.getElementById("drTestsEnabled")?.checked,testDie:v("drTestDie"),testDefaultDifficulty:v("drTestDefaultDifficulty"),
    xpPerLevel:v("drXpPerLevel"),skillPointsPerLevel:v("drSkillPointsPerLevel"),startingSkillPoints:v("drStartingSkillPoints"),
    physicalDamageStep:v("drPhysicalStep"),physicalDamageGain:v("drPhysicalGain"),
    physicalDamageFormula:document.getElementById("drPhysicalFormula")?.value||"step",physicalDamagePercentPerPoint:v("drPhysicalPctPerPoint"),
    magicDamageStep:v("drMagicStep"),magicDamageGain:v("drMagicGain"),
    magicDamageFormula:document.getElementById("drMagicFormula")?.value||"step",magicDamagePercentPerPoint:v("drMagicPctPerPoint"),
    rangedDamageFormula:document.getElementById("drRangedDamageFormula")?.value||"none",rangedDamagePercentPerPoint:v("drRangedPctPerPoint"),
    hpFormula:document.getElementById("drHpFormula")?.value||"step",hpPercentPerPoint:v("drHpPctPerPoint"),
    manaFormula:document.getElementById("drManaFormula")?.value||"step",manaPercentPerPoint:v("drManaPctPerPoint"),
    critFormula:document.getElementById("drCritFormula")?.value||"step",critPercentPerPoint:v("drCritPerPoint"),
    dodgeFormula:document.getElementById("drDodgeFormula")?.value||"step",dodgePercentPerPoint:v("drDodgePerPoint"),

    magicResistFormula:document.getElementById("drMagicResistFormula")?.value||"step",magicResistPerPoint:v("drMagicResistPerPoint"),
    meleeHitStep:v("drMeleeHitStep"),meleeHitGain:v("drMeleeHitGain"),

--- armorReductionStep in dungeonArmorReductionFromScore ---
function dungeonArmorReductionFromScore(score){
  const r=loadDungeonRpgRules();
  return Math.max(0,Math.floor(Math.max(0,Number(score)||0)/Math.max(1,r.armorReductionStep))*Math.max(0,Number(r.armorReductionGain)||0));
}

--- dungeonMitigationForHero( in rollEnemyAttack ---
function rollEnemyAttack(instanceId,overrideRule=null,attackName=""){
  const inst=loadActiveEnemies().find(x=>String(x.id)===String(instanceId));if(!inst)return;
  const def=activeEnemyDefinition(inst.enemyId);if(!def)return;
  const r=overrideRule||def.rule||{};
  if(isDungeonMode()){
    const sk=r._dungeonSkill||null,profile=dungeonEnemyAttackProfile(def,sk),rules=loadDungeonRpgRules();
    const dice=profile.dice,forced=(dungeonCombatEnemyTargetHeroId&&dungeonCombatEnemyTargetHeroId!=="__auto__")?dungeonCombatEnemyTargetHeroId:null;
    const results=[],allocations={};
    for(let i=0;i<dice;i++){
      const heroId=forced||dungeonChooseEnemyTarget();if(!heroId)continue;
      const target=dungeonDerivedForHero(heroId);
      const defPenalty=Math.max(0,(target.defense-profile.attributeValue)*rules.defensePenaltyPerPoint);
      const chance=dungeonClampHitChance(profile.preDefenseChance-defPenalty),threshold=d100ThresholdFromChance(chance),roll=Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1,hit=roll>=threshold;
      const critThreshold=Math.max(1,101-profile.crit),crit=hit&&profile.crit>0&&roll>=critThreshold;
      const raw=hit?Math.round(profile.power*(crit?rules.critMultiplier:1)):0,elementMultiplier=gensElementMultiplier(profile.element,gensHeroElementTypes(heroId)),elementRaw=Math.max(0,Math.round(raw*elementMultiplier));
      let dodgeRoll=null,dodged=false,mit=null,final=0;
      if(hit){
        if(target.dodge>0){dodgeRoll=Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1;dodged=dodgeRoll>=Math.max(1,101-target.dodge)}
        if(!dodged){mit=dungeonMitigationForHero(heroId,profile.damageType);final=Math.max(Number(mit.min)||0,elementRaw-Math.max(0,Number(mit.reduction)||0));if(elementRaw<=0)final=0;allocations[heroId]=(allocations[heroId]||0)+final;}
      }
      results.push({heroId,chance,threshold,roll,hit,crit,raw,elementRaw,elementMultiplier,dodgeRoll,dodged,mit,final,defPenalty,targetDefense:target.defense});
    }
    document.getElementById("specialDiceTitle").textContent="⚔️ "+def.name+(attackName?" — "+attackName:"");
    document.getElementById("specialDiceIcon").textContent="👹";
    document.getElementById("specialDiceMeta").innerHTML=[
      dice+" D100",(profile.mode==="magic"?"INT":profile.mode==="ranged"?"AGI":"FOR")+" "+profile.attributeValue,
      profile.baseChance+"% base + "+profile.statBonus+"% stat = "+profile.preDefenseChance+"% avant Défense",
      "Dégâts "+profile.baseDamage+" + "+profile.damageBonus+" = "+profile.power,
      "Crit "+profile.crit+"% · ×"+rules.critMultiplier,profile.damageType==="magic"?"Dégâts magiques":"Dégâts physiques",profile.element?((gensElementById(profile.element)?.icon||"🌈")+" "+(gensElementById(profile.element)?.name||profile.element)):"Neutre"
    ].map(x=>"<span>"+x+"</span>").join("");
    document.getElementById("specialDiceResult").innerHTML="🎲 <strong>Lancer en cours…</strong>";
    document.getElementById("specialDiceModal").classList.add("open");
    const animThreshold=results[0]?.threshold||d100ThresholdFromChance(profile.chance),rolls=results.map(x=>x.roll);
    animateRpgDice("specialDiceRolls",rolls.length,rolls,animThreshold,100,()=>{
      const lines=results.map(x=>{
        const name=CHARS[x.heroId]?.name||x.heroId;
        let t='<strong>'+z40kEscHtml(name)+'</strong> · D100 '+x.roll+' / '+x.threshold+'+ ('+x.chance+'%) '+(x.hit?'✅':'❌');
        t+='<br><small>Défense '+x.targetDefense+' → −'+x.defPenalty+'% toucher</small>';
        if(x.hit){t+='<br>💥 Brut '+x.raw+(x.crit?' · CRITIQUE':'')+(profile.element&&x.elementMultiplier!==1?' · 🌈 ×'+x.elementMultiplier+' = '+x.elementRaw:'');if(x.dodgeRoll!==null)t+=' · 💨 esquive D100 '+x.dodgeRoll+' / '+Math.max(1,101-dungeonDerivedForHero(x.heroId).dodge)+'+ '+(x.dodged?'✅':'❌');if(!x.dodged&&x.mit)t+='<br>🛡️ '+x.mit.label+' −'+x.mit.reduction+' → <strong>'+x.final+' dégât'+(x.final>1?'s':'')+'</strong>';}
        return '<div class="ruleEntry" style="margin:6px 0">'+t+'</div>';
      }).join('');
      const actualTotal=Object.values(allocations).reduce((n,v)=>n+Math.max(0,Number(v)||0),0);
      document.getElementById("specialDiceResult").innerHTML=lines+(actualTotal?'<strong>Total à appliquer : '+actualTotal+'</strong><br>'+dungeonAllocationText(allocations):'<strong>✅ Aucun dégât final.</strong>');
      if(actualTotal>0){dungeonPendingHeroDamage={allocations,total:actualTotal};document.getElementById("specialDiceResult").innerHTML+="<br><button type='button' style='width:100%;margin-top:10px;background:#742b27' onclick='dungeonConfirmPendingHeroDamage()'>❤️ APPLIQUER "+actualTotal+" DÉGÂT"+(actualTotal>1?"S":"")+"</button>";}else dungeonPendingHeroDamage=null;
    });
    return;
  }
  const dice=Math.max(0,Number(r.attackDice)||0);
  const rawAcc=Number(r.attackAccuracy)||0;
  if(dice<=0 || rawAcc<2 || rawAcc>6){alert("Aucun jet d’attaque n’est configuré pour cet ennemi.");return;}
  const accuracy=Math.max(2,Math.min(6,rawAcc));
  const rolls=Array.from({length:dice},()=>Math.floor(Math.random()*6)+1);
  const hits=rolls.filter(v=>v>=accuracy).length;
  const penalty=enemyAutoArmorPenalty(r);
  const damage=Math.max(1,Number(r.damage)||1);
  showEnemyAttackResolution(def,rolls,hits,accuracy,penalty,damage);
}

--- dungeonMitigationForHero( in resolveAiAttack023 ---
function resolveAiAttack023(inst,def,skill,targetHeroId){
  const profile=dungeonEnemyAttackProfile(def,skill);
  const rules=loadDungeonRpgRules();
  const target=dungeonDerivedForHero(targetHeroId);
  const dice=Math.max(1,Number(profile?.dice)||1);
  let total=0,hits=0,dodges=0,crits=0;
  for(let i=0;i<dice;i++){
    const defPenalty=Math.max(0,((Number(target.defense)||0)-(Number(profile.attributeValue)||0))*Number(rules.defensePenaltyPerPoint||0));
    const chance=dungeonClampHitChance((Number(profile.preDefenseChance)||0)-defPenalty);
    const threshold=d100ThresholdFromChance(chance),roll=Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1;
    if(roll<threshold)continue;
    hits++;
    const critThreshold=Math.max(1,101-(Number(profile.crit)||0));
    const crit=(Number(profile.crit)||0)>0&&roll>=critThreshold;if(crit)crits++;
    const raw=Math.round((Number(profile.power)||0)*(crit?Number(rules.critMultiplier||2):1));
    const mult=gensElementMultiplier(profile.element,gensHeroElementTypes(targetHeroId));
    const elemental=Math.max(0,Math.round(raw*mult));
    const dodge=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(target.dodge)||0));
    if(dodge>0 && (Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1)>=Math.max(1,101-dodge)){dodges++;continue}
    const mit=dungeonMitigationForHero(targetHeroId,profile.damageType);
    let final=Math.max(Number(mit.min)||0,elemental-Math.max(0,Number(mit.reduction)||0));
    if(elemental<=0)final=0;
    total+=Math.max(0,final);
  }
  const applied=silentHeroDamage023(targetHeroId,total);
  return {dice,hits,dodges,crits,total:applied,skill:skill?.name||"Attaque"};
}

--- dungeonMitigationForHero( in resolveReadable ---
function resolveReadable(inst,def,skill,targetHeroId){
  const profile=dungeonEnemyAttackProfile(def,skill);
  const rules=loadDungeonRpgRules();
  const target=dungeonDerivedForHero(targetHeroId);
  const dice=Math.max(1,Number(profile?.dice)||1);
  let total=0,hits=0,dodges=0,crits=0;
  const rolls=[];
  for(let i=0;i<dice;i++){
    const defPenalty=Math.max(0,((Number(target.defense)||0)-(Number(profile.attributeValue)||0))*Number(rules.defensePenaltyPerPoint||0));
    const chance=dungeonClampHitChance((Number(profile.preDefenseChance)||0)-defPenalty);
    const threshold=d100ThresholdFromChance(chance);
    const roll=Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1;
    const rd={roll,threshold,chance,hit:false,crit:false,dodged:false,damage:0};
    if(roll<threshold){rolls.push(rd);continue}
    hits++;rd.hit=true;
    const critThreshold=Math.max(1,101-(Number(profile.crit)||0));
    const crit=(Number(profile.crit)||0)>0&&roll>=critThreshold;
    if(crit){crits++;rd.crit=true}
    const raw=Math.round((Number(profile.power)||0)*(crit?Number(rules.critMultiplier||2):1));
    const mult=gensElementMultiplier(profile.element,gensHeroElementTypes(targetHeroId));
    const elemental=Math.max(0,Math.round(raw*mult));
    const dodge=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(target.dodge)||0));
    if(dodge>0 && (Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1)>=Math.max(1,101-dodge)){
      dodges++;rd.dodged=true;rolls.push(rd);continue
    }
    const mit=dungeonMitigationForHero(targetHeroId,profile.damageType);
    let final=Math.max(Number(mit.min)||0,elemental-Math.max(0,Number(mit.reduction)||0));
    if(elemental<=0)final=0;
    rd.damage=Math.max(0,final);total+=rd.damage;rolls.push(rd);
  }
  const applied=typeof silentHeroDamage023==="function"?silentHeroDamage023(targetHeroId,total):0;
  markKoIfNeeded(targetHeroId);
  return {dice,hits,dodges,crits,total:applied,skill:skill?.name||"Attaque",rolls};
}

--- dungeonMitigationForHero( in resolve036 ---
function resolve036(inst,def,skill,targetHeroId){
  const profile=dungeonEnemyAttackProfile(def,skill);
  const rules=loadDungeonRpgRules();
  const target=dungeonDerivedForHero(targetHeroId);
  const dice=Math.max(1,Number(profile?.dice)||1);
  let total=0,hits=0,dodges=0,crits=0;
  const rolls=[];

  for(let i=0;i<dice;i++){
    const defPenalty=Math.max(0,
      ((Number(target.defense)||0)-(Number(profile.attributeValue)||0))*
      Number(rules.defensePenaltyPerPoint||0)
    );
    const chance=dungeonClampHitChance((Number(profile.preDefenseChance)||0)-defPenalty);
    const threshold=d100ThresholdFromChance(chance);
    const roll=Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1;
    const rd={roll,threshold,chance,hit:false,crit:false,dodged:false,damage:0};

    if(roll<threshold){rolls.push(rd);continue}
    hits++;rd.hit=true;

    const critThreshold=Math.max(1,101-(Number(profile.crit)||0));
    const crit=(Number(profile.crit)||0)>0 && roll>=critThreshold;
    if(crit){crits++;rd.crit=true}

    const raw=Math.round((Number(profile.power)||0)*
      (crit?Number(rules.critMultiplier||2):1));
    const mult=gensElementMultiplier(profile.element,gensHeroElementTypes(targetHeroId));
    const elemental=Math.max(0,Math.round(raw*mult));

    const dodge=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(target.dodge)||0));
    if(dodge>0 && (Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1)>=Math.max(1,101-dodge)){
      dodges++;rd.dodged=true;rolls.push(rd);continue;
    }

    const mit=dungeonMitigationForHero(targetHeroId,profile.damageType);
    const reduction=Math.max(0,Number(mit.reduction)||0);
    const configuredMin=Math.max(0,Number(mit.min)||0);
    /* Une touche ayant encore une valeur >0 après l'élément ne peut pas être
       annulée totalement par l'armure. Les anciennes sauvegardes pouvaient
       conserver minPhysicalDamage/minMagicDamage à 0. */
    let final=elemental>0
      ? Math.max(1,configuredMin,elemental-reduction)
      : 0;
    rd.rawDamage=raw;
    rd.elementalDamage=elemental;
    rd.reduction=reduction;
    rd.minDamage=Math.max(1,configuredMin);
    rd.damage=Math.max(0,final);
    total+=rd.damage;
    rolls.push(rd);
  }

  let applied=0;
  if(total>0 && targetHeroId){
    try{
      const s=loadState(targetHeroId);
      const maxHp=dungeonMaxHpForHero(targetHeroId,s);
      const beforeWounds=Math.max(0,Number(s.wounds)||0);
      const beforeHp=Math.max(0,maxHp-beforeWounds);
      s.wounds=Math.min(maxHp,beforeWounds+Math.max(0,Math.round(total)));
      localStorage.setItem(key(targetHeroId),JSON.stringify(s));
      const afterHp=Math.max(0,maxHp-(Number(s.wounds)||0));
      applied=Math.max(0,beforeHp-afterHp);
      try{if(typeof z40kSchedulePush==="function")z40kSchedulePush()}catch(e){}
    }catch(e){console.warn("AI damage 0.42",e)}
  }

  return {
    dice,hits,dodges,crits,total:applied,calculatedTotal:total,
    skill:skill?.name||"Attaque",
    rolls,
    profile:{
      chance:Number(profile?.chance ?? profile?.preDefenseChance ?? 0),
      power:Number(profile?.power||0),
      crit:Number(profile?.crit||0),
      range:profile?.range ?? "—",
      damageType:profile?.damageType||"physique",
      element:profile?.element||"none"
    }
  };
}

--- dungeonMitigationForHero( in dc043ApplyEnemyAttack ---
function dc043ApplyEnemyAttack(inst,def,skill,targetHeroId){
  const profile=dungeonEnemyAttackProfile(def,skill);
  const rules=loadDungeonRpgRules();
  const target=dungeonDerivedForHero(targetHeroId);
  const dice=Math.max(1,Number(profile?.dice)||1);
  let total=0,hits=0,dodges=0,crits=0;
  const rolls=[];

  for(let i=0;i<dice;i++){
    const defPenalty=Math.max(0,
      ((Number(target.defense)||0)-(Number(profile.attributeValue)||0))*
      Number(rules.defensePenaltyPerPoint||0)
    );
    const chance=dungeonClampHitChance((Number(profile.preDefenseChance)||0)-defPenalty);
    const threshold=d100ThresholdFromChance(chance);
    const roll=Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1;
    const rd={roll,threshold,chance,hit:false,crit:false,dodged:false,damage:0};

    if(roll<threshold){rolls.push(rd);continue}
    hits++; rd.hit=true;

    const critThreshold=Math.max(1,101-(Number(profile.crit)||0));
    const crit=(Number(profile.crit)||0)>0 && roll>=critThreshold;
    if(crit){crits++;rd.crit=true}

    const raw=Math.round((Number(profile.power)||0)*
      (crit?Number(rules.critMultiplier||2):1));
    const mult=gensElementMultiplier(profile.element,gensHeroElementTypes(targetHeroId));
    const elemental=Math.max(0,Math.round(raw*mult));

    const dodge=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(target.dodge)||0));
    if(dodge>0 && (Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1)>=Math.max(1,101-dodge)){
      dodges++;rd.dodged=true;rolls.push(rd);continue;
    }

    const mit=dungeonMitigationForHero(targetHeroId,profile.damageType);
    const reduction=Math.max(0,Number(mit.reduction)||0);
    const configuredMin=Math.max(0,Number(mit.min)||0);
    const final=elemental>0?Math.max(1,configuredMin,elemental-reduction):0;

    rd.rawDamage=raw;
    rd.elementalDamage=elemental;
    rd.reduction=reduction;
    rd.damage=Math.max(0,final);
    total+=rd.damage;
    rolls.push(rd);
  }

  let applied=0;
  if(total>0){
    try{
      const s=loadState(targetHeroId);
      const maxHp=dungeonMaxHpForHero(targetHeroId,s);
      const beforeWounds=Math.max(0,Number(s.wounds)||0);
      const beforeHp=Math.max(0,maxHp-beforeWounds);
      s.wounds=Math.min(maxHp,beforeWounds+Math.max(0,Math.round(total)));
      localStorage.setItem(key(targetHeroId),JSON.stringify(s));
      applied=Math.max(0,beforeHp-Math.max(0,maxHp-(Number(s.wounds)||0)));
      try{if(typeof z40kSchedulePush==="function")z40kSchedulePush()}catch(e){}
    }catch(e){console.warn("MJ damage 0.43",e)}
  }

  return {
    dice,hits,dodges,crits,total:applied,calculatedTotal:total,
    skill:skill?.name||"Attaque",
    rolls,
    profile:{
      chance:Number(profile?.chance ?? profile?.preDefenseChance ?? 0),
      power:Number(profile?.power||0),
      crit:Number(profile?.crit||0),
      range:profile?.range ?? "—",
      damageType:profile?.damageType||"physique",
      element:profile?.element||"none"
    }
  };
}

      (crit?Number(rules.critMultiplier||2):1));
    const mult=gensElementMultiplier(profile.element,gensHeroElementTypes(targetHeroId));
    const elemental=Math.max(0,Math.round(raw*mult));

    /* Une touche ayant encore une valeur >0 après l'élément ne peut pas être
       annulée totalement par l'armure. Les anciennes sauvegardes pouvaient
       conserver minPhysicalDamage/minMagicDamage à 0. */
    let final=elemental>0
      ? Math.max(1,configuredMin,elemental-reduction)
      : 0;
    rd.rawDamage=raw;
    rd.elementalDamage=elemental;
    rd.reduction=reduction;
    rd.minDamage=Math.max(1,configuredMin);
    rd.damage=Math.max(0,final);
    total+=rd.damage;
    rolls.push(rd);
  }

  let applied=0;
  if(total>0 && targetHeroId){
    try{
      const s=loadState(targetHeroId);
      const maxHp=dungeonMaxHpForHero(targetHeroId,s);
      const beforeWounds=Math.max(0,Number(s.wounds)||0);
      const beforeHp=Math.max(0,maxHp-beforeWounds);
      s.wounds=Math.min(maxHp,beforeWounds+Math.max(0,Math.round(total)));
      localStorage.setItem(key(targetHeroId),JSON.stringify(s));
      const afterHp=Math.max(0,maxHp-(Number(s.wounds)||0));
      applied=Math.max(0,beforeHp-afterHp);
      try{if(typeof z40kSchedulePush==="function")z40kSchedulePush()}catch(e){}
    }catch(e){console.warn("AI damage 0.42",e)}
  }

  return {
    dice,hits,dodges,crits,total:applied,calculatedTotal:total,
    skill:skill?.name||"Attaque",
    rolls,
    profile:{
      chance:Number(profile?.chance ?? profile?.preDefenseChance ?? 0),
      power:Number(profile?.power||0),
      crit:Number(profile?.crit||0),
      range:profile?.range ?? "—",
      damageType:profile?.damageType||"physique",
      element:profile?.element||"none"
    }
  };
}

--- configuredMin in dc043ApplyEnemyAttack ---
function dc043ApplyEnemyAttack(inst,def,skill,targetHeroId){
  const profile=dungeonEnemyAttackProfile(def,skill);
  const rules=loadDungeonRpgRules();
  const target=dungeonDerivedForHero(targetHeroId);
  const dice=Math.max(1,Number(profile?.dice)||1);
  let total=0,hits=0,dodges=0,crits=0;
  const rolls=[];

  for(let i=0;i<dice;i++){
    const defPenalty=Math.max(0,
      ((Number(target.defense)||0)-(Number(profile.attributeValue)||0))*
      Number(rules.defensePenaltyPerPoint||0)
    );
    const chance=dungeonClampHitChance((Number(profile.preDefenseChance)||0)-defPenalty);
    const threshold=d100ThresholdFromChance(chance);
    const roll=Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1;
    const rd={roll,threshold,chance,hit:false,crit:false,dodged:false,damage:0};

    if(roll<threshold){rolls.push(rd);continue}
    hits++; rd.hit=true;

    const critThreshold=Math.max(1,101-(Number(profile.crit)||0));
    const crit=(Number(profile.crit)||0)>0 && roll>=critThreshold;
    if(crit){crits++;rd.crit=true}

    const raw=Math.round((Number(profile.power)||0)*
      (crit?Number(rules.critMultiplier||2):1));
    const mult=gensElementMultiplier(profile.element,gensHeroElementTypes(targetHeroId));
    const elemental=Math.max(0,Math.round(raw*mult));

    const dodge=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(target.dodge)||0));
    if(dodge>0 && (Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1)>=Math.max(1,101-dodge)){
      dodges++;rd.dodged=true;rolls.push(rd);continue;
    }

    const mit=dungeonMitigationForHero(targetHeroId,profile.damageType);
    const reduction=Math.max(0,Number(mit.reduction)||0);
    const configuredMin=Math.max(0,Number(mit.min)||0);
    const final=elemental>0?Math.max(1,configuredMin,elemental-reduction):0;

    rd.rawDamage=raw;
    rd.elementalDamage=elemental;
    rd.reduction=reduction;
    rd.damage=Math.max(0,final);
    total+=rd.damage;
    rolls.push(rd);
  }

  let applied=0;
  if(total>0){
    try{
      const s=loadState(targetHeroId);
      const maxHp=dungeonMaxHpForHero(targetHeroId,s);
      const beforeWounds=Math.max(0,Number(s.wounds)||0);
      const beforeHp=Math.max(0,maxHp-beforeWounds);
      s.wounds=Math.min(maxHp,beforeWounds+Math.max(0,Math.round(total)));
      localStorage.setItem(key(targetHeroId),JSON.stringify(s));
      applied=Math.max(0,beforeHp-Math.max(0,maxHp-(Number(s.wounds)||0)));
      try{if(typeof z40kSchedulePush==="function")z40kSchedulePush()}catch(e){}
    }catch(e){console.warn("MJ damage 0.43",e)}
  }

  return {
    dice,hits,dodges,crits,total:applied,calculatedTotal:total,
    skill:skill?.name||"Attaque",
    rolls,
    profile:{
      chance:Number(profile?.chance ?? profile?.preDefenseChance ?? 0),
      power:Number(profile?.power||0),
      crit:Number(profile?.crit||0),
      range:profile?.range ?? "—",
      damageType:profile?.damageType||"physique",
      element:profile?.element||"none"
    }
  };
}

function applyDamage48(heroId,damage){
  damage=Math.max(1,Number(damage)||1);
  const before=dungeonDerivedForHero?.(heroId)?.hp;
  dungeonSetHeroWounds?.(heroId,damage);
  const after=dungeonDerivedForHero?.(heroId)?.hp;
  try{DungeonCore01?.render?.()}catch(e){}
  return {before,after,damage:Math.max(0,(Number(before)||0)-(Number(after)||0))||damage};
}

function applyDungeonTurnEvent(evt,completedRound){const effect=evt?.effect||{kind:"none"};let result="";if(effect.kind==="spawn")result=dungeonEventSpawn(effect.qty||1,completedRound).text;else if(effect.kind==="damageRandom"){const id=dungeonRandomHero();if(id){const n=Math.max(1,Number(effect.value)||1),w=dungeonSetHeroWounds(id,n);result=(CHARS[id]?.name||findCustomHero(id)?.name||id)+" subit "+n+" blessure"+(n>1?"s":"")+". Blessures : "+w+"."}}else if(effect.kind==="healRandom"){const all=dungeonParticipants(),candidates=all.filter(id=>(Number(loadState(id)?.wounds)||0)>0),pool=candidates.length?candidates:all;const id=pool.length?pool[Math.floor(Math.random()*pool.length)]:null;if(id){const n=Math.max(1,Number(effect.value)||1),w=dungeonSetHeroWounds(id,-n);result=(CHARS[id]?.name||findCustomHero(id)?.name||id)+" récupère "+n+" blessure"+(n>1?"s":"")+". Blessures : "+w+"."}}else if(effect.kind==="merchant"){if(gensEconomyEnabled()&&currentRpgEconomy().merchantEventEnabled!==false){setTimeout(openMerchant,250);result="Un marchand ouvre sa boutique."}else result="Aucun marchand n’est disponible dans cet univers."}else if(effect.kind==="loot"){const d=dungeonGrantRandomLoot();result=d?((CHARS[d.heroId]?.name||findCustomHero(d.heroId)?.name||d.heroId)+" reçoit "+(d.item?.name||"un objet")+"."):"Aucun objet n’est disponible dans la pioche."}const hist=loadDungeonEventHistory();hist.push({id:evt.id,name:evt.name,category:evt.category||"",round:completedRound,at:Date.now(),result});saveDungeonEventHistory(hist);showEffectPopup(evt.icon||"🎲","ÉVÉNEMENT — "+(evt.name||"Donjon"),z40kEscHtml(evt.text||"")+(result?"<br><br><strong>"+z40kEscHtml(result)+"</strong>":""),"Entre le tour "+completedRound+" et le tour "+(Number(completedRound)+1),true);try{renderActiveEnemyButtons();renderActiveEnemies()}catch(e){}return {event:evt,result}}

--- dungeonSetHeroWounds( in exploreDungeonRoom ---
function exploreDungeonRoom(){
  const living=dungeonLivingEnemies();
  if(living.length){
    const names=living.slice(0,3).map(e=>activeEnemyDefinition(e.enemyId)?.name||"Ennemi");
    alert("Impossible d’explorer la salle suivante : "+living.length+" ennemi(s) de cette salle sont encore en vie.\n\n"+names.join(", ")+(living.length>3?"…":""));
    return;
  }

  if(!isDungeonMode()){alert("Cette action est réservée au profil Dungeon RPG.");return}
  const cfg=loadDungeonConfig(),s=loadDungeonState();
  if((Number(s.room)||0)>=cfg.rooms){
    showEffectPopup("🏆","DONJON TERMINÉ","Vous avez exploré les "+cfg.rooms+" salles du donjon.","Dungeon RPG",true);return;
  }
  s.room=Math.max(0,Number(s.room)||0)+1;
  const room=s.room;
  // Important : la salle courante doit être sauvegardée avant la création des ennemis.
  // Sinon leurs cartes étaient associées à la salle précédente et ne verrouillaient pas l'exploration.
  saveDungeonState(s);
  let kind=null;
  const explicitBossRooms=String(cfg.bossRooms||"").split(/[,; ]+/).map(Number).filter(n=>Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0);
  if(cfg.boss==="none")kind=null;
  else if(cfg.boss==="final"&&room===cfg.rooms)kind="boss";
  else if(cfg.boss==="everyN"&&room%Math.max(1,Number(cfg.bossEvery)||5)===0)kind="boss";
  else if(cfg.boss==="specific"&&explicitBossRooms.includes(room))kind="boss";
  else if(cfg.boss==="random"&&room>2&&Math.random()<(Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(cfg.bossChance)||13))/100))kind="boss";
  if(!kind){
    const w={enemy:44,ambush:12,trap:14,chest:14,rest:11,mystery:11,...(cfg.roomWeights||{})};
    const entries=[["enemy",w.enemy],["ambush",w.ambush],["trap",w.trap],["chest",w.chest],["rest",w.rest],["mystery",w.mystery]].map(([k,v])=>[k,Math.max(0,Number(v)||0)]);
    const total=entries.reduce((n,x)=>n+x[1],0)||1;let r=Math.random()*total;kind="enemy";
    for(const [k,v] of entries){r-=v;if(r<=0){kind=k;break}}
  }

  let result;
  if(kind==="enemy")result=dungeonEncounter(room);
  else if(kind==="ambush"){result=dungeonEncounter(room);result.title="⚡ EMBUSCADE — "+String(result.title||"Rencontre").replace(/^⚔️\s*Rencontre\s*—\s*/,"");result.text="<strong>⚠️ Le groupe est surpris : le combat commence immédiatement.</strong><br><br>"+result.text;}
  else if(kind==="boss")result=dungeonBossRoom(room);
  else if(kind==="trap"){
    const trap=dungeonPickTrapType(cfg),heroId=dungeonRandomHero();
    const resolved=dungeonResolveTrapAgainstHero(trap.id,heroId,false);
    result={title:"🪤 "+trap.name,text:resolved?.html||"Le piège se déclenche.",enemyQty:0,enemyName:""};
  }else if(kind==="chest"){
    const rarity=dungeonPickChestRarity(cfg),trapped=Math.random()*100<Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(cfg.chestTrapChance)||0));
    const el=addDungeonSceneElement({kind:"chest",name:"Coffre "+dungeonRarityLabel(rarity),rarity,trapped,room});
    result={title:"🎁 "+(el?.name||"Coffre découvert"),text:"Un coffre <strong>"+z40kEscHtml(dungeonRarityLabel(rarity).toLowerCase())+"</strong> est présent dans la salle."+(trapped?"<br>⚠️ Il peut être piégé.":"")+"<br><small>Ouvre-le depuis 🎛️ MJ / Ajouter à la salle.</small>",enemyQty:0,enemyName:""};
  }else if(kind==="rest"){
    dungeonParticipants().forEach(id=>dungeonSetHeroWounds(id,-1));
    result={title:"🔥 Salle de repos",text:"Tous les héros récupèrent 1 blessure.",enemyQty:0,enemyName:""};
  }else result={title:"✨ Sanctuaire mystérieux",text:"Le groupe découvre un lieu étrange. Cette salle peut servir d’objectif ou de choix narratif.",enemyQty:0,enemyName:""};

  const narrative=cfg.narration!==false?dungeonNarrativeText(kind,result.enemyName||"une créature",result.enemyQty||1,room):"";
  const map=cfg.map!==false?generateDungeonMap(kind,result.enemyQty||0):null;
  s.last={kind,room,at:Date.now(),narrative,map,title:result.title,trapId:result.trapId||null};
  saveDungeonState(s);updateGameStyleUi();renderActiveEnemyButtons();
  const mapText=map?'<div style="margin-top:8px"><strong>🗺️ Configuration de salle générée</strong><br>'+dungeonMapHtml(map)+'</div>':"";
  showEffectPopup("🏰","SALLE "+room+" / "+cfg.rooms+" — "+result.title,(narrative?'<div class="dungeonNarrative">'+z40kEscHtml(narrative)+'</div>':"")+result.text+mapText,"Dungeon RPG · difficulté "+cfg.difficulty,true);
  try{z40kSchedulePush()}catch(e){}
}
