function openGameCustomization(){
  ["setup","pregameSetup","menu","sheet","enemyManager","objectManager","zombieManager"].forEach(id=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.style.display="none"});
  const c=loadGameCustomization();
  document.getElementById("gcTurnOrder").checked=!!c.turnOrder;
  document.getElementById("gcRotateStarter").checked=c.rotateStarter!==false;
  document.getElementById("gcSharedPopup").checked=c.sharedPopup!==false;
  document.getElementById("gcIntermediate").checked=c.intermediate!==false;
  document.getElementById("gcCombatReserveReturn").checked=c.combatReserveReturn!==false;
  document.getElementById("gcAutoEnemyXp").checked=c.autoEnemyXp!==false;
  document.getElementById("gcEndHeroLabel").value=c.endHeroLabel||"FIN D’ACTIVATION";
  document.getElementById("gcPhaseName").value=c.phaseName||"PHASE ZOMBIES";
  document.getElementById("gcPhaseButton").value=c.phaseButton||"TERMINER LA PHASE ZOMBIES";
  document.getElementById("gcWaveLabel").value=c.waveLabel||"GÉNÉRER UNE APPARITION";
  document.getElementById("gcRulesComplexity").value=c.rulesComplexity||"simple";
  document.getElementById("gcInitiativeUsage").value=c.initiativeUsage||"tests";
  document.getElementById("gcInitiativeTurnMode").value=c.initiativeTurnMode||"fixed";
  document.getElementById("gcInitiativeDie").value=String(c.initiativeDie||20);
  document.getElementById("gcTestsEnabled").checked=c.testsEnabled!==false;
  document.getElementById("gcTestDie").value=String(c.testDie||20);
  document.getElementById("gcTestDefaultDifficulty").value=Number(c.testDefaultDifficulty)||10;
  renderGameCustomizationFields();
  document.getElementById("gameCustomization").style.display="block";
  window.scrollTo(0,0);
}
function renderGameCustomizationFields(){
  const enabled=document.getElementById("gcTurnOrder")?.checked;
  const f=document.getElementById("gcTurnFields");if(f)f.style.display=enabled?"block":"none";
  const i=document.getElementById("gcIntermediateFields");
  if(i)i.style.display=(enabled&&document.getElementById("gcIntermediate")?.checked)?"block":"none";
  const usage=document.getElementById("gcInitiativeUsage")?.value||"tests";
  const turn=document.getElementById("gcInitiativeTurnOptions"),tests=document.getElementById("gcUniversalTestOptions");
  if(turn)turn.style.display=(usage==="turns"||usage==="both"||usage==="custom")?"block":"none";
  if(tests)tests.style.display=(usage==="tests"||usage==="both"||usage==="custom")?"block":"none";
  const dieRow=document.getElementById("gcInitiativeDie")?.closest(".gameCfgRow");
  if(dieRow)dieRow.style.display=((usage==="turns"||usage==="both"||usage==="custom")&&document.getElementById("gcInitiativeTurnMode")?.value==="die")?"block":"none";
}
function saveGameCustomizationAndClose(){
  saveGameCustomization({
    turnOrder:document.getElementById("gcTurnOrder").checked,
    rotateStarter:document.getElementById("gcRotateStarter").checked,
    sharedPopup:document.getElementById("gcSharedPopup").checked,
    intermediate:document.getElementById("gcIntermediate").checked,
    combatReserveReturn:document.getElementById("gcCombatReserveReturn").checked,
    autoEnemyXp:document.getElementById("gcAutoEnemyXp").checked,
    endHeroLabel:document.getElementById("gcEndHeroLabel").value.trim()||"FIN D’ACTIVATION",
    phaseName:document.getElementById("gcPhaseName").value.trim()||"PHASE INTERMÉDIAIRE",
    phaseButton:document.getElementById("gcPhaseButton").value.trim()||"TERMINER LA PHASE",
    waveLabel:document.getElementById("gcWaveLabel").value.trim()||"ACTION",
    rulesComplexity:document.getElementById("gcRulesComplexity").value||"simple",
    initiativeUsage:document.getElementById("gcInitiativeUsage").value||"tests",
    initiativeTurnMode:document.getElementById("gcInitiativeTurnMode").value||"fixed",
    initiativeDie:Number(document.getElementById("gcInitiativeDie").value)||20,
    testsEnabled:document.getElementById("gcTestsEnabled").checked,
    testDie:Number(document.getElementById("gcTestDie").value)||20,
    testDefaultDifficulty:Math.max(1,Number(document.getElementById("gcTestDefaultDifficulty").value)||10)
  });
  try{
    const gc=loadGameCustomization(),dr=loadDungeonRpgRules();
    saveDungeonRpgRules({...dr,rulesComplexity:gc.rulesComplexity,initiativeUsage:gc.initiativeUsage,initiativeTurnMode:gc.initiativeTurnMode,initiativeDie:gc.initiativeDie,testsEnabled:gc.testsEnabled,testDie:gc.testDie,testDefaultDifficulty:gc.testDefaultDifficulty});
  }catch(e){}
  closeGameCustomization();
}

}

}

let gensPregameStep="profile";

function updatePregameWizard(){
  const profile=document.getElementById("pregameProfileStep");
  const heroes=document.getElementById("pregameHeroStep");
  if(profile)profile.style.display=gensPregameStep==="profile"?"block":"none";
  if(heroes)heroes.style.display=gensPregameStep==="heroes"?"block":"none";

  const p1=document.getElementById("pregameStepPillProfile");
  const p2=document.getElementById("pregameStepPillHeroes");
  const p3=document.getElementById("pregameStepPillLaunch");
  [p1,p2,p3].forEach(x=>{if(x)x.classList.remove("active","done")});

  if(gensPregameStep==="profile"){
    p1?.classList.add("active");
  }else{
    p1?.classList.add("done");
    p2?.classList.add("active");
  }

  const active=getActiveGameProfile?.();
  const captureFamily=!!active && active.gameStyle==="dungeon" && typeof gensCurrentContentFamily==="function" && gensCurrentContentFamily()==="creature";
  document.body.classList.toggle("gensCapturePregame",captureFamily);
  document.body.classList.toggle("gensAdventurePregame",!!active&&active.gameStyle==="dungeon");

  const dungeonBtn=document.getElementById("sessionDungeonSetupBtn");if(dungeonBtn)dungeonBtn.style.display=captureFamily?"none":"";
  const waveBtn=document.getElementById("sessionWaveSetupBtn");if(waveBtn)waveBtn.style.display=active?.gameStyle==="dungeon"?"none":"";
  const sessionGrid=[...document.querySelectorAll("#pregameHeroStep .sessionSetupGrid .sessionSetupBtn")];
  sessionGrid.forEach(btn=>{
    const txt=(btn.textContent||"").toUpperCase();
    if(captureFamily && txt.includes("OBJETS / PIOCHE"))btn.style.display="none";
  });

  const customRulesBtn=document.getElementById("sessionCustomRulesBtn");
  if(customRulesBtn)customRulesBtn.style.display=active?.gameStyle==="dungeon"?"none":"";
  const title=document.getElementById("pregameGameTitle");
  if(title)title.textContent=active?.name||"GenSrpG";

  if(gensPregameStep==="heroes")renderSessionReadySummary();
}
function showPregameProfileStep(){

  renderGameProfileLibrary();
  window.scrollTo(0,0);
}

}

}

function gensBuiltinDungeonHeroCompatible(id){
  id=String(id||"");
  return !!(
    typeof DUNGEON_HERO_IDS!=="undefined" &&
    DUNGEON_HERO_IDS.includes(id) &&
    String(activeGameProfileId())===String(GAME_PROFILE_DUNGEON_ID)
  );
}


function gensApplyHardHeroVisibility(){
  try{
    document.querySelectorAll(".charPick").forEach(btn=>{
      let id=String(btn.dataset.charId||btn.dataset.customHeroId||"");
      if(!id){
        const mm=(btn.getAttribute("onclick")||"").match(/openChar\(['"]([^'"]+)['"]\)/);
        if(mm)id=String(mm[1]);
      }
      if(!id)return;
      if(gensHardExcludedHeroForCurrentFamily(id)){
        btn.style.display="none";
        btn.setAttribute("data-gens-hard-hidden","1");
      }else if(btn.getAttribute("data-gens-hard-hidden")==="1"){
        btn.style.display="";
        btn.removeAttribute("data-gens-hard-hidden");
      }
    });
  }catch(e){}
}
function gensHardExcludedHeroForCurrentFamily(id){
  id=String(id||"");
  return !!(
    typeof DUNGEON_HERO_IDS!=="undefined" &&
    DUNGEON_HERO_IDS.includes(id) &&
    String(activeGameProfileId())!==String(GAME_PROFILE_DUNGEON_ID)
  );
}
function gensHeroCompatibleWithCurrentFamily(id){
  id=String(id||"");
  if(!id)return false;
  if(typeof DUNGEON_HERO_IDS!=="undefined" && DUNGEON_HERO_IDS.includes(id)){
    return String(activeGameProfileId())===String(GAME_PROFILE_DUNGEON_ID);
  }
  const h=findCustomHero?.(id);
  if(h?.gameMode==="dungeon")return gensContentCompatible(h,"hero");
  return !isDungeonMode();
}
function currentAllHeroIds(){
  applyCustomHeroesMulti();
  if(typeof ensureDungeonContent==="function")ensureDungeonContent();

  if(isDungeonMode()){
    const ids=new Set(customHeroesForMode(true).map(h=>String(h.id)));
    if(String(gensCurrentUniverseId())===String(GAME_PROFILE_DUNGEON_ID) && typeof DUNGEON_HERO_IDS!=="undefined"){
      DUNGEON_HERO_IDS.forEach(id=>ids.add(String(id)));
    }
    return [...ids].filter(gensHeroCompatibleWithCurrentFamily);
  }

  const ids=new Set(
    Object.keys(CHARS).filter(id=>
      !(typeof DUNGEON_HERO_IDS!=="undefined" && DUNGEON_HERO_IDS.includes(String(id)))
    )
  );
  customHeroesForMode(false).forEach(h=>ids.add(String(h.id)));
  return [...ids];
}
function captureCurrentGameProfile(name,id,builtIn=false){
  const waveId=activeWavePresetId();
  const wavePreset=loadWavePresets().find(p=>p.id===waveId);
  return {
    id:id||gameProfileId(),
    name:name||"Profil de partie",
    builtIn:!!builtIn,
    gameStyle:"custom",
    modules:{rules:true,objects:true,heroes:true,enemies:true,waves:true},
    rules:{...loadGameCustomization()},
    deck:{...loadDeckConfig()},
    heroPool:currentAllHeroIds(),
    objectPool:ITEMS.map(it=>String(it.id)),
    wavePresetId:waveId||ZOMBICIDE_BASE_PRESET_ID,
    enemyConfig:{...loadZombieConfig()},
    enemyReserve:{...(wavePreset?.reserve||loadZombieConfig())},
    enemyLoot:{enabled:false,multiplier:0.1},
    createdAt:Date.now()
  };
}
function ensureCustomContentGameModes(profiles){
  const ps=Array.isArray(profiles)?profiles:[];
  const dp=ps.find(p=>p.id===GAME_PROFILE_DUNGEON_ID);
  const dungeonObjectHints=new Set((dp?.objectPool||[]).map(String));
  const dungeonHeroHints=new Set((dp?.heroPool||[]).map(String));

  let eqChanged=false;
  const eq=loadCustomEquipment();
  eq.forEach(it=>{
    if(!it || it.gameMode)return;
    it.gameMode=dungeonObjectHints.has(String(it.id))?"dungeon":"zombicide";
    eqChanged=true;
  });
  if(eqChanged)saveCustomEquipment(eq);

  let heroChanged=false;
  const heroes=loadCustomHeroesMulti();
  heroes.forEach(h=>{
    if(!h || h.gameMode)return;
    h.gameMode=dungeonHeroHints.has(String(h.id))?"dungeon":"zombicide";
    heroChanged=true;
  });
  if(heroChanged)saveCustomHeroesMulti(heroes);
}

function ensureBaseGameProfile(){
  let profiles=loadGameProfilesRaw();
  let changed=false;
  ensureCustomContentGameModes(profiles);

  if(!profiles.some(p=>p.id===GAME_PROFILE_BASE_ID)){
    const base=captureCurrentGameProfile("Zombicide — Base",GAME_PROFILE_BASE_ID,true);
    base.gameStyle="zombicide";base.survival=gensReferenceSurvivalSettings();
    base.factory=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify({...base,factory:undefined}));
    profiles.unshift(base);
    changed=true;
  }

  if(!profiles.some(p=>p.id===GAME_PROFILE_DUNGEON_ID)){
    const dungeon=captureCurrentGameProfile("Dungeon RPG — Démo",GAME_PROFILE_DUNGEON_ID,true);
    dungeon.gameStyle="dungeon";dungeon.rpgUniverse=gensReferenceRpgSettings();
    dungeon.modules={rules:true,objects:true,heroes:true,enemies:true,waves:false};
    dungeon.rules={
      ...defaultGameCustomization(),
      turnOrder:true,
      rotateStarter:false,
      sharedPopup:true,
      intermediate:false,
      combatReserveReturn:true,
      autoEnemyXp:true,
      endHeroLabel:"FIN DU TOUR",
      phaseName:"EXPLORATION",
      phaseButton:"CONTINUER",
      waveLabel:"EXPLORER UNE SALLE"
    };
    dungeon.enemyLoot={enabled:true,multiplier:1};
    dungeon.factory=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify({...dungeon,factory:undefined}));
    profiles.push(dungeon);
    changed=true;
  }

  // Isolation stricte des contenus : Zombicide ne doit jamais récupérer un objet Dungeon.
  if(typeof ensureDungeonContent==="function")ensureDungeonContent();
  const bp=profiles.find(p=>p.id===GAME_PROFILE_BASE_ID);
  if(bp){
    bp.name="40K";
    bp.gameStyle="zombicide";
    const zItems=itemsForMode(false).map(it=>String(it.id));
    bp.objectPool=[...zItems];
    const oldDeck={...(bp.deck||{})};
    bp.deck={...defaultDeckConfig()};
    Object.keys(bp.deck).forEach(id=>bp.deck[id]=zItems.includes(String(id))?Math.max(0,Number(oldDeck[id]??bp.deck[id])||0):0);
    // Isolation stricte : aucun héros Dungeon (base ou personnalisé) dans Zombicide.
    const dungeonCustomHeroIds=new Set(customHeroesForMode(true).map(h=>String(h.id)));
    bp.heroPool=currentAllHeroIds().filter(id=>
      !(typeof DUNGEON_HERO_IDS!=="undefined"&&DUNGEON_HERO_IDS.includes(String(id))) &&
      !dungeonCustomHeroIds.has(String(id))
    );
    // Réserve/configuration 40K : uniquement les ennemis Survie.
    const oldEnemyCfg={...(bp.enemyConfig||{})},oldEnemyRes={...(bp.enemyReserve||{})};
    bp.enemyConfig={};bp.enemyReserve={};
    enemiesForMode(false).forEach(z=>{
      bp.enemyConfig[z.id]=Math.max(0,Number(oldEnemyCfg[z.id]??z.defaultCount)||0);
      bp.enemyReserve[z.id]=Math.max(0,Number(oldEnemyRes[z.id]??bp.enemyConfig[z.id])||0);
    });
    changed=true;
  }
  const dp=profiles.find(p=>p.id===GAME_PROFILE_DUNGEON_ID);
  if(dp){
    dp.name="Dungeon";
    const ids=dungeonContentIds();
    dp.gameStyle="dungeon";
    const dungeonCustomHeroIds=customHeroesForMode(true).map(h=>String(h.id));
    dp.heroPool=[...ids.heroes,...dungeonCustomHeroIds];
    const dungeonCustomIds=customEquipmentForMode(true).filter(it=>!it.dungeonBuiltin).map(it=>String(it.id));
    dp.objectPool=[...ids.objects,...dungeonCustomIds];
    dp.modules={rules:true,objects:true,heroes:true,enemies:true,waves:false};
    const oldDungeonDeck={...(dp.deck||{})};
    dp.deck={...defaultDeckConfig()};
    Object.keys(dp.deck).forEach(id=>dp.deck[id]=dp.objectPool.includes(String(id))?Math.max(0,Number(oldDungeonDeck[id]??1)||0):0);
    dp.deck.dng_heal_potion=Math.max(3,Number(oldDungeonDeck.dng_heal_potion)||0);dp.deck.dng_mana_potion=Math.max(2,Number(oldDungeonDeck.dng_mana_potion)||0);dp.deck.dng_torch=Math.max(3,Number(oldDungeonDeck.dng_torch)||0);
    const oldDungeonEnemyCfg={...(dp.enemyConfig||{})},oldDungeonEnemyRes={...(dp.enemyReserve||{})};
    dp.enemyConfig={};dp.enemyReserve={};
    enemiesForMode(true).forEach(z=>{
      dp.enemyConfig[z.id]=Math.max(0,Number(oldDungeonEnemyCfg[z.id]??z.defaultCount)||0);
      dp.enemyReserve[z.id]=Math.max(0,Number(oldDungeonEnemyRes[z.id]??dp.enemyConfig[z.id])||0);
    });
    dp.dungeonConfig={...defaultDungeonConfig(),...(dp.dungeonConfig||{})};
    changed=true;
  }
  if(changed)saveGameProfiles(profiles);
  return profiles;
}
function loadGameProfiles(){
  // Ne reconstruire les profils que s'ils manquent réellement.
  // Avant, chaque simple lecture réécrivait les pools et localStorage, ce qui
  // créait lenteurs et états intermédiaires lors des changements 40K/Dungeon.
  const raw=loadGameProfilesRaw();
  const hasBase=raw.some(p=>p?.id===GAME_PROFILE_BASE_ID);
  const hasDungeon=raw.some(p=>p?.id===GAME_PROFILE_DUNGEON_ID);
  return (hasBase&&hasDungeon)?raw:ensureBaseGameProfile();
}

--- dungeonSetHeroWounds( in applyEnemyConfiguredAbilityEffect ---
function applyEnemyConfiguredAbilityEffect(inst,def,ability){
  if(!ability||ability.effectKind==="none")return "";
  const chance=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(ability.chance??100)));
  const roll=Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1;
  if(roll>chance)return "Effet raté ("+roll+" > "+chance+").";

  const raw=ability.effectValue;
  const val=Math.max(0,Number(raw)||0);
  const duration=Math.max(1,Number(ability.duration)||1);
  const tick=Math.max(0,Number(ability.tickValue)||0);
  const mode=ability.effectValueMode||"flat";
  const kind=ability.effectKind;
  const heroes=typeof dungeonParticipants==="function"?dungeonParticipants():[];
  const heroTargets=dungeonAbilityHeroTargets(ability);

  if(kind==="heal_self"){
    const max=Math.max(1,Number(inst.maxHp)||Number(inst.hp)||1);
    const heal=dungeonAbilityResolvedValue(raw,mode,max);
    const before=Number(inst.hp)||0;inst.hp=Math.min(max,before+heal);
    return "Récupère "+(inst.hp-before)+" PV.";
  }

  if(kind==="heal_all_enemies"){
    const list=loadActiveEnemies();let total=0,count=0;
    list.forEach(e=>{
      if(e.defeated||Number(e.hp)<=0)return;
      const max=Math.max(1,Number(e.maxHp)||Number(e.hp)||1);
      const heal=dungeonAbilityResolvedValue(raw,mode,max),before=Number(e.hp)||0;
      e.hp=Math.min(max,before+heal);total+=e.hp-before;count++;
    });
    saveActiveEnemies(list);
    return count+" ennemi(s) soigné(s), "+total+" PV restaurés.";
  }

  inst.runtimeMods=inst.runtimeMods||{actions:0,strength:0,armor:null};
  if(kind==="bonus_damage"){inst.runtimeMods.strength=(Number(inst.runtimeMods.strength)||0)+val;return "+"+val+" dégâts pendant "+duration+" tour(s)."}
  if(kind==="bonus_armor"){inst.runtimeMods.armor=Math.max(0,Number(def.rule?.armor)||0)+val;return "+"+val+" Armure pendant "+duration+" tour(s)."}
  if(kind==="bonus_actions"){inst.runtimeMods.actions=(Number(inst.runtimeMods.actions)||0)+val;return "+"+val+" action(s)."}

  if(kind==="damage_hero"||kind==="damage_team"){
    const targets=kind==="damage_team"?heroes:(heroTargets.length?heroTargets:[]);
    const dmg=Math.max(1,val);
    targets.forEach(id=>dungeonSetHeroWounds(id,dmg));
    return targets.length+" héros touché(s), "+dmg+" dégâts "+(ability.damageType||"physical")+".";
  }

  if(["poison","burn","bleed"].includes(kind)){
    const targets=heroTargets.length?heroTargets:[];
    const label={poison:"Poison",burn:"Brûlure",bleed:"Saignement"}[kind];
    targets.forEach(id=>{
      if(val>0)dungeonSetHeroWounds(id,val);
      addDungeonHeroStatus(id,{kind,label,remaining:duration,tick:Math.max(1,tick||val||1),damageType:ability.damageType||kind,stackable:!!ability.statusStackable,source:def?.name||"Ennemi"});
    });
    return label+" : "+val+" dégât(s) immédiat(s), puis "+Math.max(1,tick||val||1)+" / tour pendant "+duration+" tour(s).";
  }

  if(kind==="stun"){
    const targets=heroTargets.length?heroTargets:[];
    targets.forEach(id=>addDungeonHeroStatus(id,{kind:"stun",label:"Étourdi",remaining:duration,tick:0,stackable:false,source:def?.name||"Ennemi"}));
    return targets.length+" héros étourdi(s) pendant "+duration+" tour(s).";
  }

  if(kind==="reduce_armor"||kind==="reduce_damage"){
    const targets=heroTargets.length?heroTargets:[];
    const label=kind==="reduce_armor"?"Armure réduite":"Dégâts réduits";
    targets.forEach(id=>addDungeonHeroStatus(id,{kind,label,remaining:duration,value:val,stackable:!!ability.statusStackable,source:def?.name||"Ennemi"}));
    return label+" de "+val+" pendant "+duration+" tour(s).";
  }

  if(kind==="drain_mana"){
    const targets=heroTargets.length?heroTargets:[];
    targets.forEach(id=>{const st=loadState(id);st.mana=Math.max(0,(Number(st.mana)||0)-val);localStorage.setItem(key(id),JSON.stringify(st));});
    return targets.length+" héros : -"+val+" Mana.";
  }

  if(kind==="lifesteal"){
    const targets=heroTargets.length?heroTargets:[];
    const dmg=Math.max(1,val);let dealt=0;
    targets.forEach(id=>{dungeonSetHeroWounds(id,dmg);dealt+=dmg});
    const max=Math.max(1,Number(inst.maxHp)||Number(inst.hp)||1),before=Number(inst.hp)||0;
    inst.hp=Math.min(max,before+dealt);
    return dealt+" dégâts infligés · "+(inst.hp-before)+" PV volés.";
  }

  if(kind==="spawn"){
    const enemyId=String(raw||"").trim();if(!enemyId)return "ID ennemi manquant.";
    const qty=Math.max(1,Number(ability.duration)||1);
    const created=trackSpawnedEnemyInstances(enemyId,qty);
    return created.length+" ennemi(s) invoqué(s).";
  }
  return "";
}

--- dungeonSetHeroWounds( in applyDungeonHeroStatusRoundEffects ---
function applyDungeonHeroStatusRoundEffects(){
  if(!isDungeonMode())return;
  const all=loadDungeonHeroStatuses(),notes=[];
  Object.keys(all).forEach(heroId=>{
    const arr=Array.isArray(all[heroId])?all[heroId]:[];
    const next=[];
    arr.forEach(st=>{
      if(["poison","burn","bleed"].includes(st.kind)&&Number(st.tick)>0){
        dungeonSetHeroWounds(heroId,Number(st.tick));
        notes.push((CHARS[heroId]?.name||heroId)+" : "+st.label+" -"+st.tick+" PV");
      }
      st.remaining=Math.max(0,(Number(st.remaining)||1)-1);
      if(st.remaining>0)next.push(st);
    });
    all[heroId]=next;
  });
  saveDungeonHeroStatuses(all);
  if(notes.length)setTimeout(()=>showEffectPopup("☠️","EFFETS PERSISTANTS",notes.map(z40kEscHtml).join("<br>"),"Début du nouveau tour",true),180);
}

--- dungeonSetHeroWounds( in dungeonResolveTrapAgainstHero ---
function dungeonResolveTrapAgainstHero(trapId,heroId,show=true){const trap=dungeonTrapTypes()[trapId]||dungeonTrapTypes().dart;if(!heroId)return null;const chance=dungeonTrapCheckChance(heroId,trap),threshold=101-chance,roll=Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1,ok=roll>=threshold;let wounds=null;if(!ok)wounds=dungeonSetHeroWounds(heroId,Math.max(1,Number(trap.damage)||1));const name=CHARS[heroId]?.name||findCustomHero(heroId)?.name||heroId,val=dungeonTrapStatValue(heroId,trap.stat),html='<strong>'+z40kEscHtml(name)+'</strong> tente de résister au piège.<br>'+trap.statLabel+' '+val+' → <strong>'+chance+'%</strong> de réussite · D100 : <strong>'+threshold+'+</strong><br>Jet : <strong>'+roll+'</strong> → '+(ok?'✅ <strong>RÉUSSI</strong>':'❌ <strong>ÉCHEC</strong>')+(ok?'':('<br>💥 '+trap.damage+' dégât'+(trap.damage>1?'s':'')+' · blessures actuelles : '+wounds));if(show)showEffectPopup(trap.icon,"PIÈGE — "+trap.name,html,'Test '+trap.statLabel+' · '+chance+'%',true);return {ok,roll,chance,threshold,html}}

function explore(){if(!canAct()){modal('⏳ Action refusée','Ce n’est pas le tour du héros contrôlé par ce téléphone.');return}const living=dungeonLivingEnemies?.()||[];const exitLocked=living.length>0 && (typeof window.dungeonRoomExitLocked102==="function"?window.dungeonRoomExitLocked102():true);if(exitLocked){modal('🔐 Sortie verrouillée','La sortie de cette salle est verrouillée.  let result;if(kind==='enemy'){

En mode tactique, il ne se déclenche qu’au contact de sa case.',enemyQty:0,enemyName:'',trapId:trap.id}}else if(kind==='chest'){const rarity=dungeonPickChestRarity(cfg),trapped=Math.random()*100<Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(cfg.chestTrapChance)||0));const el=addDungeonSceneElement({kind:'chest',name:'Coffre '+dungeonRarityLabel(rarity),rarity,trapped,room});result={title:'🎁 '+(el?.name||'Coffre découvert'),text:'Un coffre <strong>'+z40kEscHtml(dungeonRarityLabel(rarity).toLowerCase())+'</strong> est présent dans la salle.'+(trapped?'<br>⚠️ Il peut être piégé.':''),enemyQty:0,enemyName:''}}else if(kind==='merchant'){const el=addDungeonSceneElement({kind:'merchant',name:'Marchand itinérant',room});result={title:'🧙‍♂️ Marchand itinérant',text:'Un marchand a installé son étal dans cette salle. Le groupe peut commercer avant de repartir.',enemyQty:0,enemyName:''}}else if(kind==='rest'){

  });

}else result={title:'✨ Salle calme',text:'La salle est vide. Le groupe peut souffler avant de poursuivre.',enemyQty:0,enemyName:''};const narrative=cfg.narration!==false?dungeonNarrativeText(kind,result.enemyName||'une créature',result.enemyQty||1,room):'';const map=cfg.map!==false?generateDungeonMap(kind,result.enemyQty||0):null;s.last={kind,room,at:Date.now(),narrative,map,title:result.title};saveDungeonState(s);try{dc077MaybeCreateObjective?.(room,kind)}catch(e){}const mapText=map?'<div class="dc01Map"><strong>🗺️ Configuration</strong><br>'+dungeonMapHtml(map)+'</div>':'';try{queueDungeonRoomEvent074?.(room,kind)}catch(e){}const ambushAction=kind==='ambush'

  :'';

render();
}

<script id="dungeonCore303TimelineRootFix">
(function(){
"use strict";

/* Core 3.03 = base 2.14 conservée.
   Cette couche ne touche ni aux pions, ni aux coffres, ni aux énigmes,
   ni au renderer visuel 2.14. Elle répare uniquement la timeline. */

let T={sig:"",order:[],index:0,aiBusy:false,pendingHero:false};

function sig(){
  return JSON.stringify({
    h:(dungeonCombatSelection?.heroes||[]).map(String).sort(),
    e:(dungeonCombatSelection?.enemies||[]).map(String).sort()
  });
}
function heroAlive(id){
  try{return Number(dungeonCombatHeroSnapshot?.(id)?.hp)>0}catch(e){return false}
}
function enemyAlive(id){
  try{
    const e=(loadActiveEnemies?.()||[]).find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id));
    return !!e&&!e.defeated&&Number(e.hp)>0
  }catch(e){return false}
}
function build(){
  const a=[];
  (dungeonCombatSelection?.heroes||[]).forEach(id=>{
    if(!heroAlive(id))return;
    const sn=dungeonCombatHeroSnapshot?.(id)||{};
    a.push({kind:"hero",id:String(id),name:CHARS?.[id]?.name||String(id),initiative:Number(sn.initiative)||0})
  });
  try{
    (loadActiveEnemies?.()||[])
      .filter(e=>(dungeonCombatSelection?.enemies||[]).map(String).includes(String(e.id))&&!e.defeated&&Number(e.hp)>0)
      .forEach(e=>{
        const d=activeEnemyDefinition?.(e.enemyId);
        const dr=dungeonEnemyDerivedForInstance?.(e)||dungeonEnemyDerived?.(d)||{};
        a.push({kind:"enemy",id:String(e.id),name:d?.name||e.name||"Ennemi",initiative:Number(dr.initiative)||0})
      });
  }catch(e){}
  a.sort((x,y)=>y.initiative-x.initiative||(x.kind==="hero"?-1:1));
  return a;
}
function init(force=false){
  const s=sig();
  if(!force&&T.sig===s&&T.order.length)return;
  T={sig:s,order:build(),index:0,aiBusy:false,pendingHero:false};
}
function current(){
  init();
